(4) mēlum, vaṭamoḻiyiliruntu tōṉṟiyaṉavē tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ eṉṟu nampiya mēṉāṭṭārāycci yāḷarkaḷukku, vaṭa coṟkaḷiyē eṭuttāḷāmalō aṅkoṉṟum iṅkoṉṟumāka mikavum arukiya niliyilmaṭṭum eṭuttāṇṭō varum tiruntā moḻikaḷum tirāviṭa moḻi yiṉattil irukkiṉṟaṉa eṉpatē teriyātu. aṉṟiyum, vaṭacohṟkaḷi ōraḷaviṟku eṭuttāḷum tiruntiya tirāviṭa moḻikaḷum kaṭṭāyamāka avaṟṟi eṭuttāḷattāṉ vēṇṭumeṉpatillāmal, viruppamuḷatēṟ payaṉpaṭuttiyum, iṉṟēl aṟavē yotukkiyum varukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatum avarkaḷukkut teriyātu. āṉāl, iṉṟiya niliyi nōkkiṉ, vaṭamoḻiyi aṟavē nīkkuvateṉpatu teluṅku moḻikku aritākum; kaṉṉaṭa moḻikkō mikavum aritu; maliyāḷa moḻikkō aritiṉumaritu. im moḻikaḷ kaṇakku vaḻakkillāmal vaṭa coṟkaḷi eṭuttāṇṭu vantuḷḷami yālum, ac coṟkaḷiṉ utaviyi nāṭuvatē vaḻakkamākak koṇṭuḷḷamiyālum, tattam ciṟappup paṇpukaḷi yiḻantu, taṉittu niṉṟiyaṅkum āṟṟaliyum iḻantu niṟkiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ aṉittiṉum mikavum tiruntiya paṇpaṭṭa niliyiluḷḷa tamiḻmoḻiyō, vēṇṭumeṉṟāl, vaṭacoṟkaḷi aṟavē akaṟṟit taṉittiyaṅkum āṟṟal vāyntiruppa tōṭaṉṟi, avaṟṟiṉ utavi yillāmal mikavum mēmpaṭṭu, vaḷamuṟṟu miḷirum āṟṟalum vāyntatākum.
paṇṭit tūya tamiḻmoḻikkuc centamiḻ eṉṟu peyar. ic centamiḻilēyē tamiḻmoḻiyiṟ kāṇappaṭum paṇṭiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷiṟ perumpālaṉa iyaṉṟuḷḷaṉa. āṉāl, avaṟṟuḷ kāṇappaṭum vaṭacoṟkaḷō mikamikak kuṟivu. iṉṟiya pēccut tamiḻuṭaṉum, urinaṭit tamiḻuṭaṉum innili māṟupaṭṭatoṉṟākak kāṇappaṭum. etaṉāl avvāṟeṉilō, kaṇṇuṅ karuttumāy iruntu vaṭacoṟ kaḷiyum, vaṭamoḻi yeḻuttukkaḷiyum vilakki, tūya tamiḻccoṟkaḷ, marapumoḻikaḷ, ākiyavaṟṟiyē kiyāṇṭu vantuḷḷamiyiṉālētāṉ. tamiḻmakkaḷiṭiyē ic centamiḻ moḻip pātukāppu evvaḷavukkup paraviyirunta teṉpatu, eḻutappaṭṭa oru tamiḻnūlil evvaḷavuk kevvaḷavu vaṭa coṟkaḷ arukik kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉavō, avvaḷavukku avvaḷavu atu ciṟantatoru nūleṉṟum, evvaḷavuk kevvaḷavu vaṭacoṟkaḷ mikutiyākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉavō avvaḷavukkavvaḷavu atu tāḻntatoru nūleṉṟum karuti matippiṭum paḻakkam paṇṭutoṭṭup payiṉṟu varukiṉṟami yiṉālēyē iṉitu teḷiyappaṭum. piṟamoḻi nūlkaḷ cilavaṟṟil evvaḷavuk kevvaḷavu vaṭamoḻic coṟkaḷ payiṉṟu varukiṉṟaṉavō avvaḷavukkavvaḷavu annūlkaḷ avvammoḻiyiṉarāṟ ciṟappuṭaṉ pōṟṟappaṭum; tamiḻilō evvaḷavukkevvaḷavu tamiḻ nūlkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyiṉ utaviyi nāṭāmal taṉittiyaṅkukiṉṟaṉavō avvaḷavuk kavvaḷavu  ciṟappuṭaṉ pōṟṟappaṭum. uṇṇāṭṭuc ciṟṟūrkaḷilum, nāṭṭuppuṟaṅkaḷilum vāḻntuvarum tāḻnta makkaḷiṭiyē vaṭamoḻic coṟkaḷip pēccuvaḻakkilum kiyāḷāmal otukkum tūya paḻakkam kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. oru moḻiyiṉ toṉmic ciṟappunili am moḻiyiyalilum ceyyuḷ kaḷilum, tāḻnta kuṭimakkaḷiṉ pēccukaḷilumiruntē ārāyntu kāṇappaṭum eṉpatu orupotu uṇmiyākum. piṟkālattil mikavum valintu muyaṉṟu eḻutappaṭṭa tamiḻ urinaṭi nūlkaḷilum, pārppaṉarkaḷ pēcum tamiḻilum, mikavum kaṟṟavar kaḷākak karutappaṭum tamiḻarkaḷiṉ pēccilumē vaṭamoḻi mikipaṭap paravi vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. atuvum, camaya uṇmi, aṟiviyal, tattuvam, ēṉik kalikaḷiluḷḷa marapuc coṟkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟi viḷakkumiṭaṅkaḷil maṭṭumē ivvāṟu vaṭacoṟ kaḷ perumpāṉmiyum kiyāḷappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl, ivvāṟu kāṇappaṭum vaṭa coṟkaḷiṉ toki, āṅkila nūlkaḷiṟ kiyāḷappaṭṭuk kāṇappaṭum ilattīṉ moḻic coṟkaḷiṉ tokiyi viṭa mikutiyāṉatoṉṟaṉṟu.
eṭuttukkāṭṭāka, āṅkila viviliya nūlilum, tamiḻ viviliya nūlilum “pattuk kaṭṭaḷikaḷ” eṉṟa pakutiyi oppumikkāka eṭuttukkoṇṭu avaṟṟiṉ col tokuti kaḷi ārāyvōm. āṅkilac coṟkaḷi ārāyumiṭattu nēriṭiyāṉa ilattīṉ coṟkaḷiyum, nārmaṉ pireñcu mūlamāka vanta ilattīṉ citivukaḷiyum oṉṟākac cērttu ilattīṉākak kaṇakkiṭuvōm. ataṉ palaṉ kīḻvarum paṭṭikippaṭi yākum.
col vaki	āṅkilam	tamiḻ
	mottam	ilattīṉ	tūya        mottam	  vaṭa	taṉit				āṅkilam		moḻi	tamiḻ
peyarum	43	14	29	53	21	32
peyaruriyum	20	7	13	34	7	27
viṉi
eṇṇup
peyar	5	0	5	6	1	5
“āyiram” eṉpatu vaṭamoḻip peyar. ataṉālēyē eṇṇup peyari ṉetiril vaṭamoḻiyiṉ kīḻ `1’ eṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.
mottamākak kaṇakkiṭṭāl āṅkilattil varum ilattīṉum, tamiḻil varum vaṭamoḻiyum orēpaṭittāka nūṟṟukku 45 viḻukkāṭu ākiṟatu. iraṇṭilum iṭappeyarkaḷ, uriccoṟkaḷ, urupukaḷ, iṭiccoṟkaḷ, ilakkaṇa uṟuppukkaḷ ivi muṟṟilum tāymoḻiyic cērntaviyē yākum.

āṅkilamoḻi taṉ vaḷarccimuṟiyil tūya āṅkilō cāksaṉ moḻiyiliruntum,  ilattīṉ moḻiyiliruntum evvakiyil utavipeṟṟirukkiṟatu eṉpati āṅkila camayat talivar ṭreñc7 piṉvarumāṟu viḷakkik kūṟiyuḷḷār.
“im moḻiyi (āṅkilam) ṉuṭiya iṇippukaḷ, oli muṟi, nāṭi, narampukaḷ, taci nārkaḷ, iṭaccoṟkaḷ, urupukaḷ, iṭiccoṟkaḷ, eṇṇup peyarkaḷ, tuṇi viṉikaḷ, yāppu muṟikkup payaṉpaṭum ciṟuciṟu acic coṟkaḷ, ilakkaṇa muṟiyamippu ākiyavi ellām taṉippaṭṭa āṅkilō cāksaṉē. it teyvikak kaṭṭiṭattiṟku ilattīṉ moḻi utaviyavi ceṅkaṟkaḷum, naṉku cetukkik kaṭicaliṭap paṭṭa calavik kaṟkaḷumēyām; āṉāl ivi yellāvaṟṟiyum patittup piṇittuk kaṭṭiṭamāka eḻuppuvikka utaviya nīṟu, cuṇṇāmpu, cāntu ellām tūya āṅkilō cāksaṉē”.

ikkūṟṟu appaṭiyē tirāviṭattiṟkum vaṭamoḻikkum poruntuvatākum; mēṟ pakutiyil “āṅkilō cāksaṉ” eṉṟu varumiṭaṅkaḷi lellām, “tirāviṭam” eṉpatiyum, “ilattīṉ” eṉṟu varumiṭaṅkaḷilellām “vaṭamoḻi” eṉpatiyum amittukkoṇṭu paṭittāl tirāviṭa moḻikkum vaṭamoḻikkum iṭiyē ettuṇi yuṟavu irukkiṟa teṉpatu teḷḷat teḷiya viḷaṅkum.
mēṟ “pattuk kaṭṭaḷikaḷ” eṉṟa tamiḻ viviliya nūṟpakutiyil kāṇappaṭum vaṭacoṟṟokiyum āṅkila nūṟpakutiyil kāṇappaṭum ilattīṉ coṟṟokiyum viyakkattakka vaṇṇam oṉṟākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟateṉpati aṟintōm. ataṉāl āṅkilam ilattīṉukku evvaḷaviṟkuk kaṭaṉpaṭṭuḷḷatō, avvaḷaviṟkut tamiḻ vaṭamoḻikkuk kaṭaṉpaṭṭirukkavēṇṭum eṉṟu colvatu tavaṟākum. āṅkilap pakuti taṉmāṭṭuḷḷa ilattīṉ coṟkaḷi nīkkiviṭa muyaṉṟāl, ataṉ poruḷ nuṭpaṅ keṭṭuviṭum. āṉāl, tamiḻp pakuti taṉmāṭṭuḷḷa vaṭacoṟkaḷil perum pakutiyiyō, aṉṟi muḻuvatiyumō nīkkiviṭṭut takka tamiḻc coṟkaḷip peytukoṇṭāl evvakiyiluṅ kuṟivuṟātu; avvāṟu nīkkuvatāl ataṉ tūymi māṟṟum, cemmip paṇpum uyarntoḷirumēyaṉṟik kuṟintu maṅkā ilattīṉi liruntu peṟṟa coṟkaḷiṟ palavaṟṟiṟku āṅkilō cāksaṉil nērc coṟkaḷ kiṭiyā; eṉavē, ac coṟkaḷi nīkkiviṭuva teṉṟāl avaṟṟiṉ karuttukkaḷi nīṇṭa, aḻakaṟṟa coṟ ṟoṭarkaḷāl tāṉ viḷakkavēṇṭum. āṉāl tamiḻō, taṉippaṭṭa vakiyil colvaḷam niṟintatu. appaṭiyirukka a/tēṉ vaṭacoṟkaḷi eṭuttāṇṭa teṉṟāl, colvaḷa miṉmiyāṉaṉṟu; putumi karutiyēyām eṉṟu viṭukka. “pattuk kaṭṭaḷikaḷ” eṉṟa viviliya nūṟpakutiyi nāṭṭuppuṟat tāḻnta makkaḷiṉ pēccu muṟiyil amittāl, atiluḷḷa vaṭacoṟkaḷiṉ toki mikavuṅ kuṟintuviṭum. ataṉiyē cemmippaṭuttit tirutti ilakkiya naṭiyil amippō māṉāl, atil eñciyuḷḷa oṉṟiraṇṭu vaṭacoṟkaḷum māyamāyp pōyviṭum. appakutiyiṟ kāṇappaṭum vaṭa coṟkaḷuḷ oṉṟē oṉṟiṟkuttāṉ kuṟṟañ collāta vakiyil nērt tamiḻccol amikka muṭiyātu. ac col “vikkirakam”8 eṉpatu. ac collum ataṉ karuttum tamiḻmakkaḷiṉ paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷukkum, koḷkikaḷuṭakkum puṟampāṉavi. pārppaṉarkaḷāl purāṇak karuttukaḷōṭum, “vikkiraka ārātaṉi muṟi” eṉṉum uruva vaḻipāṭṭōṭum, ic collum karuttum tamiḻnāṭṭiṉuḷ pukuttappaṭṭaṉa vākum. pārppaṉavarkaḷiṉ camayattiṟku in nāṭṭil mutalil irunta celvākkiṉālēyē, camayak karuttukkaḷit terivippaṉavāyt tamiḻnūṟkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum coṟkaḷiṟ perumpālaṉa vaṭacoṟkaḷākavō, accoṟ citivu kaḷākavō kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ēṟṟa tirāviṭac coṟkaḷ illi eṉpatilli; muṟṟilum ēṟṟaviyum, orōvaḻi vaṭacoṟkaḷilum ciṟantaṉavākavuṅ koḷḷak kūṭiya coṟkaḷ uḷa. avi yellām, iṉṟu vaḻakkiḻantu, maṟintu, ceyyuḷ vaḻakkilmaṭṭum aruki vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻk kalvi maṅkiyuḷḷa innāḷil, urinaṭiyil ac coṟkaḷ vaḻaṅkumēl an naṭi mikavum karaṭumuraṭāṉa teṉṟum, kaṭapaṭām pōṉṟa teṉṟum karuti ikaḻappaṭum. tamiḻc camaya nūlkaḷil vaṭacoṟkaḷ potuvāka eṭuttāḷappaṭṭuḷḷamikku ituvē, uṇmi yāṉa kāraṇamum, orē kāraṇamumākum.
ēṉiya tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil epporuḷik kuṟitteḻunta kaṭṭuriyāyiṉuñ cari, ceyyuḷāyiṉuñ cari, ataṉkaṇ mikutiyāṉa vaṭacoṟkaḷ payiṉṟirukkak kāṇalām. avi putumi karuti eṭuttāḷappaṭṭaṉavalla; kiṭṭattaṭṭa iṉṟiyamiyātaṉavākavē karutik kiyāḷap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. inta nilimi vantataṟkuk kāraṇam ammoḻikaḷiṉ ilakkiya vaḷarcci mutaṉmiyākap pārppaṉarkaḷ kiyilēyē oppaṭikkappaṭṭu vantuḷḷamiyēyām. teluṅku moḻiyi yeṭuttukkoṇṭāl, am moḻiyil ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ eḻutiyavarkaḷuḷḷum, ilakkiyaṅkaḷ eḻutiyavarkaḷuḷḷum mutaṉmiyāṉavarkaḷum, peyar pōṉavarkaḷum pārppaṉarkaḷē. ammoḻiyiṟ kāṇappaṭum nūlkaḷuḷ oṉṟē oṉṟutāṉ pārppaṉa rallātārāṉa āciriyaroruvarāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭa tākum. uṇmiyākap pārttāl, teluṅkumakkaḷ eṉṟu aḻippataṟku evvakiyilum uriyarāṉa teluṅkup pārppaṉa rallāta tokutiyiṉar taṅkaḷ moḻivaḷarcci, kalivaḷarcci ākiyavaṟṟip pārppaṉarkaḷiṭamē oppaṭittu viṭṭavar kaḷākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟārkaḷ.
tamiḻilō ippaṭiyilli. nūl eṉṟa peyarukkuriyaṉa vāyp pōṟṟic cēmittuvikkavēṇṭiya vakiyil pārppaṉar kaḷāl eḻutappaṭṭaṉavāyk kāṇakkūṭiyavi mikamikac cilavē. tamiḻmakkaḷāl avarkaḷuṭiya tāy moḻiyākiya tamiḻiṉ vaḷarcciyum, kaliyum, nākarikamum paṇṭutoṭṭuk kaṇṇuṅ karuttumāy ārvattuṭaṉum veṟṟiyuṭaṉum pōṟṟappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻ ilakkiya āciriya variciyil pārppaṉarkaḷ eytiyatellām uriyāciriyar eṉṟa nilikku mēṟpaṭṭa tilli. tāḻnta vakuppiṉarāya tiruvaḷḷuvarāl eḻutappaṭṭa tirukkuṟaḷukkup pārppaṉarāṉa parimēlaḻakar eṉpār vakuttuḷḷa uriyē mikavuñ ciṟantatoṉṟākap pārāṭṭap paṭukiṟatu.
teṉpakutiyil pēcappaṭum moḻikaḷ vaṭamoḻiyip piṉpaṟṟiyē amikkappaṭṭapōtilum, taṉippaṭṭa ilakkiya vaḷatti ēṟpaṭuttikkoḷḷa avaṟṟuṭ cila virumpi muyaṉṟaṉa eṉṟum, tamiḻ, teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭam, maliyāḷam ākiya moḻikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum mutaṉmiyāṉa ilakkiyaṅka ḷellām vaṭamoḻi nūlkaḷiṉ moḻi peyarppō, aṉṟi virivuriyō ātalvēṇṭum eṉṟum, av virivuriyeḻutu vatilum vaṭamoḻit teṭarkaḷ eṭuttāḷappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa eṉṟum pērāciriyar uvilsaṉ9 eṉpavar kūṟukiṟār. tamiḻippaṟṟiya variyil ik kūṟṟu oppattakkataṉṟu. tamiḻil iraṇṭu oppaṟṟa ciṟanta nūlkaḷāka ulakattōrāl karutappaṭuvaṉa kuṟaḷum, cintāmaṇiyumām. iv viraṇṭum muṟṟilum vaṭamoḻit toṭarpaṟṟavi. tamiḻāciriyarkaḷ vaṭamoḻi nūlamippiyum naṭiyiyum piṉpaṟṟiyirukkak kūṭum; toṭarukkut toṭar moḻipeyarttuḷḷaṉar eṉpati oppuk koḷḷa muṭiyātu. tamiḻ irāmāyaṇam, māpāratam pōṉṟa kāviyaṅkaḷ ivvāṟu eḻuntavēyām. tamiḻar taṅkaḷ kamparāmāyaṇam mutaṉūlākiya vālmīki irāmāyaṇattiṉum mikavuñ ciṟantatu eṉṟu urimi pārāṭṭuvatē itaṟkuc cāṉṟākum.
(5) moḻikaḷuḷ oṉṟaṟkoṉṟaṉiṭiyē kāṇappaṭum oṟṟumi vēṟṟumikaḷi viḷakkap peritum payaṉpaṭum muṟikaḷ mikavum muṭivāṉatu am moḻikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇa amippi oppiṭṭuc cīrtūkkip pārppatēyām. am muṟippaṭi tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ vaṭamoḻiyiṉum vēṟāṉavi yeṉṟum, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷi intu -airōppiya moḻiyiṉattōṭu iṇippativiṭa, cittiya moḻiyiṉat tōṭu iṇippatē cālpuṭittākum eṉṟum tuṇintu kūṟalām.
aṭikkuṟippukaḷ
1.	ṉucayeciyaḷa.
2.	ḻuyaracayaḷa
3.	aiṉēṭi-nuracaṭiniyan.
4.	ḷuulavāyin.
5.	kuinniḷā
6.	ṉuiuviṭiyēcaniḷa.
7.	hacuhbiḷāṭiyī kūcananuh.
8.	aiayapana.
9.	ñacaṭikanaḷaḷaṭica ṟuṭiḷaṭin

5. ilakkaṇa amippil vaṭamoḻikkum tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukkum iṭiyēyuḷḷa vēṟupāṭukaḷ
(i) tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil uyiraṟṟa poruḷkaḷ maṭṭumaṉṟi, pakuttaṟivaṟṟa uyirkaḷum potuppāl (a/ṟiṇi) ākavē matikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. āṇpāl, peṇpāl vakuppu, iṭap peyarkaḷ, atilum paṭarkkiyil maṭṭumē, kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. peyaruric coṟkaḷilum, paṭarkki viṉikaḷilum ip paṭarkkip peyariṉ tiripē vikutiyāy niṉṟu pāl uṇarttukiṉṟatu. uyirvakikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ pālvaki kāṭṭavēṇṭiṉ āṇ peṇ eṉṟa peyarkaḷic cērttēyākavēṇṭum; appōtum avi āṇ allatu peṇ pālāvatilli; potuppālēyākum. ēṉeṉil, avaṟṟik kuṟikkum iṭappeyarkaḷum, avaṟṟiṉ payaṉiliyāṉa  viṉikaḷum potuppāl (a/ṟiṇi) muṭiviyē koḷḷalvēṇṭum. ittakiya pālvakuppu vaṭamoḻi mutaliya intu - airōppiya moḻikaḷiṉ pakaṭṭāṉa puṉiviyal pālpākupāṭṭiṉiṉṟum muṟṟilum māṟupaṭṭatu. āṉāl cittiyak kuḻuviṉ vaḻakkukku i/tu ottatu.

(ii) tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil peyarc coṟkaḷ vēṟṟumi ēṟkumpōtu vikutiyēṟṟu urumāṟuva tilli; ac coṟkaḷiṉiṉṟu eḷitil pirikkakkūṭiya piṉ aṭikaḷ allatu urupukaḷiyē ēṟṟu māṟukiṉṟaṉa. mēlum paṉmip peyariṉ vēṟṟumi yuruvāṉatu urupukaḷi ēṟkumuṉ paṉmi vikuti peṟuvatoṉṟit tavira maṟṟevvakiyilum orumi vēṟṟumi yuruviliruntu māṟupaṭuvatilli; cittiya moḻikaḷum ivvāṟē.1
(iii) tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ a/ṟiṇip peyarkaḷukkup paṉmi perumpālum vaḻaṅkuvatilli; viṉic collil i/tu iṉṉum arumi.
(ie) tirāviṭa moḻiyiṉ nāṉkām vēṟṟumi yurupākiya ku-ki-allatu ke vaṭamoḻi allatu intu - airōppiya (āriya) moḻikaḷiluḷḷa enta vēṟṟuimi vikutiyuṭaṉum ottatāka illi. āṉāl atē camayattil atu kīḻit turukkiyuṭaṉum2, pehistaṉ3 paṭṭayaṅkaḷuṭaṉum piṉṉiṣ moḻiyiṉaṅkaḷuṭaṉum toṭarpuṭiya tāyirukkiṉṟatu.
(e) intu-airōppiya moḻikaḷil muṉṉurupukaḷ4 allatu oṭṭukkaḷ (pirattiyayam) payaṉpaṭuttappaṭum iṭaṅkaḷil ellām tirāviṭa moḻikaḷum, cittiyak kuḻumoḻikaḷum piṉṉurupukaḷi allatu oṭṭukkaḷip payaṉpaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa. iv urupukaḷum, oṭṭukkaḷum ilakkaṇa muṟippaṭi taṉiccolvakikaḷ alla, peyarccol tiripukaḷēyām. ivvāṟē avaṟṟiṉ viṉi uriccoṟkaḷum peyar allatu toḻiṟ peyar allatu viṉiyeccaṅkaḷē yākum. avi eppōtum tām taḻuvum viṉikaḷukku muntiyē varukiṉṟaṉa.
(ei) vaṭamoḻiyilum, intu - airōppiya moḻikaḷilum peyaruric coṟkaḷ peyarc coṟkaḷip pōlavē pāl, eṇ, iṭam ēṟkiṉṟaṉa. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷilum, cittiya moḻikaḷilum avi pāl mutaliyavi ēṟpatilli. ip peyaruric coṟkaḷ uṇmiyil paṇpup peyarkaḷiliruntu vantaviyē yākum. paṇpup peyar eṉṟu taṉippaṭa niṟkumpōtu, avi piṟa peyarkaḷ pōlavē tiṇi pāl mutaliyavi ēṟpiṉum, piṟa peyarkaḷuṭaṉ oṭṭappeṟṟu uriccollākap payaṉpaṭumpōtu aṅṅaṉam ēṟpatilli.
(eii) iṉṉoru vakiyil tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ maṅkōliyam, mañcu mutalāṉa cittiya moḻikaḷuṭaṉ ottum intu - airōppiya moḻiyīṉattuṭaṉ māṟupaṭṭum uḷḷaṉa. a/tāvatu, iyalumpō tellām im moḻikaḷ peyaruric colli vaḻaṅkāmal, viṉiccolliṉ peyarecca urupiyē vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. (uyar maram - eṉpataṟku uyarnta maram eṉpatupōla eṉka.) ivvaḻakkattiṉ payaṉāyp paṇpup peyarkaḷip peyaruriyāka vaḻaṅkumpōtu kūṭa, ataṉuṭaṉ peyaruriccol vikutiyāka `āṉa’ eṉṟa peyareccam cērttukkoḷḷappaṭukiṟatu. (civappāṉa)
(eii) taṉmip paṉmiyil muṉṉiliyi uṭpaṭuttiyum uṭpaṭuttāmalum iraṇṭu vakikaḷ tirāviṭa moḻikkum, cittiya moḻikaḷukkumē ciṟappāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. vaṭamoḻi yilum, intu -airōppiya moḻiyilum iṅṅaṉam illi. itaṉi yoppatāy oruvāṟu kuṟikkakkūṭiyatu piṟkuṟitta moḻikaḷiluḷḷa irumi5 eṉṟa eṇṇēyākum.
(io) tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil ceyappāṭṭu viṉikkut taṉi vaṭivam kiṭiyātu. paṭu eṉṉum tuṇiviṉi cērkkappaṭṭē ap poruḷil vaḻaṅkappaṭukiṟatu.
(o) tirāviṭa moḻikaḷum, cittiya moḻikaḷum intu - airōppiya moḻikaḷip pōla iṇippu iṭic coṟkaḷip6 payaṉpaṭuttāmal “ceytu” eṉṟa viṉiyeccattiyē vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
(oi) uṭaṉpāṭu, etirmaṟi eṉṟa iv viraṇṭu viṉi vaṭivaṅkaḷiruppatu tirāviṭa moḻikaṭkum, vaṭamoḻikkum iṭiyē kāṇappaṭum taliyāya vēṟṟumikaḷuḷ oṉṟu. cittiya moḻikaḷuṭaṉ tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukku uḷḷa oppumikaḷil itu talimiyāṉatākak kāṇkiṟatu.
(oii) tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ, maṅkōliyam, mañcu, mutaliyavi, ōraḷavukkup piṟa cittiya moḻikaḷ ākiya ivaṟṟiṉ taṉippaṭṭa ciṟappup paṇpukaḷuḷ oṉṟu iṇippu iṭappeyarkaḷiṉiṭamākap peyareccaṅkaḷ7 vaḻaṅkuvatām. ippeyareccam mukkālaṅkaḷilum uḷḷa viṉiyeccattuṭaṉ āṟām vēṟṟumi urupōṭotta a-eṉṉum cāriyi peṟṟu vantatu ākum. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ etaṉilum iṇippu iṭappeyar eṉpataṉ niḻalkūṭak kāṇappaṭavilli. kōṇṭu moḻiyil maṭṭum viṉiyeccaṅkaḷ illāmal oḻintu pōṉati oṭṭi, hinti moḻiyiṉ iṇippu iṭappeyar kiyāḷappaṭṭu varukiṟatu.

(oiii) tirāviṭa moḻikaḷilum, cittiya moḻikaḷilum taḻuvu moḻikaḷum, taḻuvu toṭarkaḷum taḻuvappaṭum moḻikkup piṉṉatākavē varukiṉṟaṉa. intu - airōppiya moḻi iṉattil avi potuvāka muṉvarukiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē, im moḻikaḷ aṉittilum vākkiyattiṉ mutalil eḻuvāyum, muṭivil payaṉiliyum, eḻuvāy aṭi8 eḻuvāykku muṉṉum, iṭiyil ceyappaṭuporuḷum viṉi aṭikaḷum ākavarukiṉṟaṉa; peyaraṭikaḷum peyareccaṅkaḷum peyariṉ āṟām vēṟṟumiyum peyarukku muṉṉum, iṭa urupukaḷum kāla urupukaḷum peyarukku muṉ aṉṟit tām taḻuvum peyarkaḷukkum avaṟṟiṉ vēṟṟumi urupukaḷukkum piṉṉākavum varukiṉṟaṉa. viṉi yeccaṅkaḷum viṉi aṭikaḷ pōla viṉikku muṉṉākiṉṟaṉa. ellāvaṟṟiyum taḻuvum viṉimuṟṟu iṟutiyilēyē niṟkiṟatu. ivvāṟu collamippu, coṟṟoṭaramippu muṟiyilum cittiya moḻikaḷ tirāviṭa moḻikaḷi ottē irukkiṉṟaṉa.
mēṟkūṟiya ilakkaṇa amitikaḷilellām tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ potuvāka intu - airōppiya moḻikaḷiṉiṉṟum vēṟupaṭṭē iruppatum, neṭunāḷ kalantu vāḻntuṅkūṭa vaṭamoḻiyiṉiṉṟum iṉṉum vēṟupaṭṭē yiruppatum kāṇka. atē camayattil, eṅkeṅkō naṭu āciyāvilum, vaṭāciyāvilum citaṟik kiṭakkum cittiya moḻikaḷuṭaṉ avi porunti iruppatu kavaṉikkat takkatu.
tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ vēṟu, vaṭamoḻi vēṟu eṉpatu mēṟkūṟiyavaṟṟāl naṉku teriyappaṭum. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ vaṭamoḻiyiliruntu peṟappaṭṭaṉa alla; aṉṟi ataṉuṭaṉ cērkkappaṭakkūṭiya paṇpuṭiyaṉavumalla.
cila kuṟippiṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷil vaṭamoḻi piṟa intu - airōppiya moḻikaḷiviṭac cittiya moḻik kuḻuvuṭaṉ uṟavuṭiyatāyt tōṟṟuvatu oru vēḷip paḻaṅkuṭikaḷuṭaṉ uḷḷa neṭunāṭ paḻakkattip poṟuttatāyirukkalām. ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu, vaṭamoḻiyīl (ṭa ṇa ṣa) eṉṟa nāaṭi aṇṇa meykaḷ iruppaṉavākum. iv vakiyil, “moḻiyiyalil cīṉattiṉ iṭam”9 eṉṟa nūlil eṭkiṉs eṉpār koṇṭa putiya koḷkiyik kuṟippiṭal vēṇṭum. urupiṉṟi vikutiyilēyē peyarkaḷiṉ vēṟṟumi amital colloḻuṅku, taḻuvu vākkiyaṅkaḷ talivākkiyattiṟku muṉvaral, viṉimuṟṟu iṟutiyil niṟṟal ākiya ilakkaṇa amippukkaḷuḷ vaṭamoḻi āriya iṉattiviṭat turāṉiya iṉattiyē piṉpaṟṟiyuḷḷatu eṉa avar kūṟukiṟār. āṉāl, vaṭamoḻiyi viṭa ivvakiyil iṉṟiya vaṭa intiya uṇṇāṭṭu moḻikaḷē vilakkiṉṟi muṟṟilum iv voḻuṅkip piṉpaṟṟukiṉṟaṉa. vaṭamoḻi urinaṭiyilkūṭa oṉṟiraṇṭiṭaṅkaḷil iv voḻuṅkukku māṟupāṭu ēṟpaṭukiṟateṉpati eṭkiṉsē eṭuttukkāṭṭukiṟār. āyiṉum potuppaṭa urinaṭiyil iv voḻuṅkuppaṭi viṉimuṟṟē īṟṟilum, taḻuvu vākkiyaṅkaḷ muṉṉum varukiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl, ik koḷki nām ēṟṟukkoḷḷattakka uṟutippāṭu uṭiyataṉṟu; ēṉeṉil col oḻuṅku oṉṟiyēpaṟṟi vakippaṭuttuvatāyiṉ kirēkka moḻiyiviṭa ilattīṉum, āṅkilattiviṭa jermaṉum turāṉiyac cārpuṭiyavi eṉṟu kūṟavēṇṭi varum.
aṭikkuṟippukaḷ
1.	vaṭamoḻiyilum, intu-airōppiya moḻikaḷilum orumikkum, paṉmikkum ovvoru vēṟṟumiyilum vevvēṟu vikutikaḷ uḷa.
2.	ṭīcanivēyaṭakūracamiḷā.
3.	kṣanāḷivaran kūyaṭenavaḷa.
4.	ñacanayīṭiḷaviiṭin
5.	orumi paṉmi eṉpatiṉṟi orumi, irumi, paṉmi eṉṟu eṉ mūṉṟu vaḻaṅkum. 
6.	ūṭitēranuviṭiḷē.
7.	cunaṭayaviena ñacaṭinṭiraḷē.
8.	haṉataranuvaḷa.
9.	“ūhiyē’ḷa yīṭayuna in ñāṭiṭiṭaṭipala” - āca. nuṉamiḷē.
6. tirāviṭamum vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷum
tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ vaṭamoḻiyiliruntu peṟappaṭṭaviyē, avaṟṟuḷ vaṭamoḻic cārpaṟṟaṉavākak kāṇakkiṭakkum coṟkaḷ yātō oru paṇṭip pākata moḻiyiliruntu peṟappaṭṭavi eṉṟu koṇṭirunta koḷkikaḷ iraṇṭum piḻipaṭṭaṉa veṉṟu kāṭṭappaṭṭatu. eṉavē, aṟiñar cilar ivaṟṟiṟku nērmāṟāṉa koḷki yoṉṟik kūṟa muṉvantaṉar. vaṭamoḻiyiliruntu kiḷitta vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷuḷ kāṇappeṟum piṟamoḻik kalapput tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ kūṭṭuṟavāl ēṟpaṭṭatutāṉ eṉpatu ip putuk koḷki. ik koḷkiyip parappiyavarkaḷil taliyāṉōr pampāyic cārnta rereṇṭ ṭākṭar sṭīveṉcaṉ1 eṉpavarum, nēpāḷattic cārnta hāṭjsaṉ2 eṉparuvamē. ivarkaḷ karuttu (1) vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷ iyaṟkit tākkutalkaḷālum, kāla māṟupāṭṭālum vaṭamoḻiyiliruntu kiḷittaviyalla; āṉāl, vaṭamoḻiyallāta piṟa moḻikaḷiṉ āṟṟal mikka kalappiṉāl citivuṟṟaṉavēyām. (2) ip piṟamoḻikaḷ tirāviṭap pēccuṭaṉ oppumiyuṭiyaṉa; it tirāviṭap pēccē, paṇṭi niṣātarkaḷ pēccum, piṟa paḻaṅkālak kuṭikaḷiṉ pēccumākum eṉpatē.
ivviru pakutikaḷuḷ muṉṉiyatu piṉṉiyativiṭa uṟutippāṭuṭiyatākum; āṉāl atuvum oruvāṟu mikipaṭak kūṟappaṭṭateṉṟum, ataṉit tiruttiyamikka vēṇṭum eṉṟum tōṉṟukiṉṟatu. ēṉeṉiṟ kūṟutum: mutaṉmiyāṉa cilavaṟṟi nōkka vaṭamoḻi hintiyākavum, vaṅkāḷamākavun tiripuṟṟatu iyaṟki māṟutalē eṉṟu niṟuvappaṭṭuḷḷatu. airōppāvil ilattīṉiliruntu eppaṭi ittāliyamum, spāṉiṣum kiḷittaṉavō, appaṭiyētāṉ iviyum. āṉāl, vaṭamoḻi ilakkaṇa amippōṭum paṇpuc ciṟappukkaḷōṭum, vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇa amippiyum paṇpuc ciṟappukkaḷiyum oppiṭṭu nōkkiṉāl, avaṟṟiṭiyē kāṇappaṭum vēṟṟumikaḷ āriyac cārpaṟṟa tākkutalkaḷiṉ payaṉē eṉṟu oruvāṟu teḷiyalām.
atilum, palvēṟupaṭṭa vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷu ḷellām orēpaṭittāṉa ilakkaṇa vēṟṟumikaḷ kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟami koṇṭu, itaṟkuk kāraṇam potuppaṭṭa oru tākkutalākavē irukkavēṇṭum eṉpatu eḷitil ūkikkap paṭum. “nākarika kālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭa paḻaṅkālac cuvaṭukaḷākiya paṇṭiya, cemmippaṭāta muraṭṭu moḻiccoṟkaḷē” itaṟkuk kāraṇam eṉṟu pērāciriyar uvilsaṉ kūṟukiṟār. potuvākavē, vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷuḷ ittakiya tākkutal pattil orupaṅkum, marātti moḻiyil maṭṭum aintil orupaṅkuntāṉ eṉṟu kaṇakkiṭappaṭṭuḷḷamiyāl, āriyac cārpaṟṟa kalappē kāraṇam eṉpatu naṉku teḷiyappaṭum.
vaṭamoḻi pēciya pārppaṉarkaḷ, kṣattiriyarkaḷ, viciyarkaḷ eṉṟa vakuppiṉarkaḷaṭaṅkiya āriyarkaḷ varukikku muṉṉē, vaṭa intiyāviṉ perum pakutiyil “tasyūkkaḷ, niṣātar, milēccar” eṉṟu avarkaḷāl aḻikkappaṭṭu vanta paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷē vāḻntu vantiruntaṉar eṉpatum, am makkaḷ cittiya iṉattiyō, aṉṟi āriyac cārpaṟṟa vēṟōr iṉattiyō cērntavarkaḷ eṉpatum aṉivarālum oppukkoḷḷappaṭṭa uṇmikaḷākum. ip paḻaṅkuṭimakkaḷ pēciya tiruntāta cittiya moḻic, coṟkaḷ, paṭiyeṭuttu vantu veṟṟipeṟṟa āriyarkaḷ tam colvaḷamum, poruṇmiyum niṟinta vaṭamoḻi veḷḷattil āḻntu pōyviṭutal iyalpē. eṉiṉum, ap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷiṉ moḻi orēyaṭiyāy aḻintoḻiyāmal, taṉṉi viḻuṅkavanta vaṭamoḻiyiyē uraṅkoṇṭu tākkik kalakkit taṉṉuṭiya coṟkaḷiṟ cilavaṟṟip pukuttip putiyatoru matukiyiyum naṭip pōkkiyum atu mēṟkoḷḷac ceytirukkavēṇṭu meṉpatu piṉvaruṅ kāraṇaṅkaḷāl ūkikkappaṭum: (1) cittiya moḻiyiṉ ilakkaṇa amippu niliyāṉatum, etirppukku īṭukōṭukkum āṟṟalum vāyntatu. (2) āriyarkaḷi nōkka ap paḻaṅkuṭikaḷ tokiyil mikuntavarkaḷā yiruntaṉar. (3) ataṉāl, avarkaḷi āriyarāl aṟavē aḻittoḻikka muṭiyavilli. (4) mutaṟkaṇ aṭimikaḷākkappaṭṭu nāḷā vaṭṭattil āriyak kūṭṭattiṉaruṭaṉē cērkkappaṭṭaṉar.
ik koḷki maṟṟek koḷkikiyum viṭa iṉṟiya nilimiyit teḷivupaṭa viḷakkukiṟatu. vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷuḷ coltokutiyilmaṭṭumē vaṭa moḻic cārpu mikutiyāyiruppatum, ilakkaṇa amippu cittiya moḻic cārpuṭiyatā yiruppatum, nōkka., ammoḻikaḷ cittiyap pakutiyuṭaṉ kalanta āriya aṭippaṭiyuṭiyavi eṉpativiṭa, āriyattuṭaṉ kalanta cittiya aṭippaṭiyi uṭiya moḻikaḷ eṉṟu kūṟuvatē mikavum poruttamuṭiyatākum. im moḻikaḷuḷ “tāttāriya allatu cālṭiya”3 (atāvatu cittiya)k kalappu uḷḷatu eṉa mutaṉmutal kūṟiyavar car. uvilliyam jōṉs4 āvar. itaṉi maṟuppavaruḷ taliyāṉavar hinti moḻiyip paṟṟi, 1872 ēpril iṇṭiyaṉ āṇṭikkavari5 eṉṟa veḷiyīṭṭil kaṭṭuri oṉṟeḻutiya kirus pi.si.es6. āvar. avar hintimoḻi oṉṟi maṭṭum ārāyntuḷḷār; atilum ilakkaṇa amippi viṭṭuc col tokuti oṉṟi maṭṭumē ārāyntuḷḷār. ākiyāl iṉṉum ittuṟiyil nuṇuki ārāyntu muṭipu kāṇum variyil, cittiya aṭippaṭi muṭipē nilavi varum.
iṉi, ṭākṭar sṭīveṉsaṉ koḷkiyiṉ piṉṉiya pakutiyi nōkkuvōm. vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷil vaṭa moḻiyallāta piṟa moḻik kalappu irukkiṟateṉpatu oppukkoḷḷappaṭiṉ, ap piṟamoḻi tirāviṭa moḻi yiṉattic cērntatē eṉpatu ap pakutiyākum. ik koḷki avvaḷavu tiṭṭamāka ārāyntu niṟuvappaṭuvatoṉṟaṉṟu. (vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷāyiṉuñ cari, teṉṉintiya moḻi kaḷāyiṉuñ cari,) ellāvaṟṟuḷḷum, cittiya moḻik kalappō, tirāviṭa moḻik kalappō perumpālum orē paṭittāṉatu. (2) āriyarāl mutalil kippaṟṟap peṟṟa vaṭa intiyak kōṭṭaṅkaḷaḷ ak kalappiṉ aḷavu mikavuṅ kuṟukiyatu. (3) caṟṟuc cēymiyiluḷḷa  takkāṇam, teliṅkāṇam, micūr ākiya iṭaṅkaḷil ak kalappiṉ aḷavu caṟṟup perukiyatu. (4) nila mūkkiṉ teṉkōṭiyil iruppatum, maṉuviṉ kālattilum, irāmāyaṇa kālattilum pārppaṉa ātikkam kārttikip piṟipōl iruntuvarappeṟṟatumākiya tamiḻ nāṭṭilō atu mikap parantu perukiyiruntatu.
mutaṟkaṇ, immuṭipu paṇṭiya intiya varalāṟṟiṉ pōkkōṭu ottatākavē kāṇappaṭum. eṉiṉum, moḻi yārāyccik kaṇṇāṭikoṇṭu pārkkiṉ vaṭa intiyap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷum, teṉṉintiyap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷum orē iṉattār eṉṟu colliviṭuvataṟkilli. vaṭintiya moḻikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇa amippu cittiya moḻikaḷōṭu ottiruppatāṉālum, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷuṭaṉ ciṟappāka ottiruppatākak kāṇappaṭavilli. vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷuḷ kāṇappeṟum  vaṭamoḻic cārpaṟṟa pakuti tirāviṭa moḻi kaḷōṭu evvaḷavu toṭarpuṭiyatāk kāṇappaṭukiṟatō, avvaḷaviṟkuk kīḻit turukkiya moḻikaḷuṭaṉō, aṉṟic cittiya moḻikaḷuṭaṉōṭu toṭarpuṭiyatākavuṅ kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ātalāl mēṟkoḷki eḷitil oppuk koḷḷak kūṭiyatoṉṟāka illi.
vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇa amippiṟkum, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇa amippiṟkum uḷḷa talimiyāṉa oppumikaḷ kīḻvarupaviyām:
(1) peyarccoṟkaḷiṉpiṉ urupukaḷ cērntu vēṟṟumikaḷ uṇṭātal. (2) paṉmip peyarkaḷiṉ vēṟṟumi orumiyiṉiṉṟum vēṟupaṭāmal paṉmi vikutiyi mutalil cērttup piṉ vēṟṟumi urupukaḷic cērppataṉāl uṇṭātal. (3) vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷ palavaṟṟuḷ tirāviṭa moḻikaḷip pōlavē taṉmip paṉmiyiṉ muṉṉiliyi uḷappaṭutti oṉṟum, vilakki oṉṟumāka iraṇṭu vaṭivaṅkaḷ iruttal. (40 urupukaḷum, oṭṭukkaḷum peyarukkumuṉ varāmal piṉvarutal. (5) viṉiccolliṉ kālatti iṭinili mutaliya uṟuppukaḷ kāṭṭal. (6) taḻuvu vākkiyaṅkaḷ talimi vākkiyattiṉ muṉ varutal. (7) taḻuvum moḻi taḻuvappaṭum moḻikkup piṉ varutal.
vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷ tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ ākiyavaṟṟiṉ ilakkaṇa amippil ittakiya oppumikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatu uṇmiyē. āṉāl, ivaṟṟāl iv viraṇṭu moḻiyiṉaṅkaḷukkum nērāṉa uṟavu uḷateṉak kūṟiviṭutaṟkilli. ēṉeṉil, iv voppumikaḷ tirāviṭa moḻikaḷōṭumaṭṭuma ciṟappākak kāṇappaṭum oṉṟumik kaḷākāmal, cittiya iṉattic cērnta moḻiyiṉaṅkaḷ palavaṟṟōṭum, orē vakiyil, orē aḷavil kāṇappaṭum potuppaṭiyāṉa oppumikaḷākiṉṟaṉa. turukkiya maṅkōliya moḻikaḷukkum, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukkum iṭiyē kāṇap paṭum vēṟṟumikaḷil yātoṉṟum, vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭavilli. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka, cittiya moḻiyiṉaṅ kaḷilillāta peyareccatti eṭuttuk koḷvōm. itu koṭu oṉṟu nīṅkalāka ēṉiya tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ ellā vaṟṟilum uḷatu; āṉāl vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷ oṉṟilum illi.
atip pōṉṟē, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil ellā viṉittiripukaḷukkum oḻuṅkāka etirmaṟiviṉi irukkiṉṟatu. avaṟṟuḷ etuvum vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷil illi. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum iṭappeyar eṇṇup peyarkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟutāṉum avaṟṟuḷ illi. āṉāl, intiyāvukku appāl pehistaṉ paṭṭaṅkaḷilum cīṉa moḻiyilum, ostiyakku7 moḻiyilum, lāppar8 moḻi yiluṅ kūṭa ivaṟṟuṭ cila kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. mēlum, im moḻikaḷil tirāviṭat tākku ētēṉum iruntirukkak kūṭumāyiṉ ki, kāl, kaṇ, kātu (cevi) pōṉṟa mutaṉmiyāṉa cila coṟkaḷēṉum, avaṟṟiṉ citivukaḷēṉum ivaṟṟuḷ em moḻiyilāyiṉum kāṇappaṭāmalirukka iṭamilli. uṇmiyil attakiya ec collum ivaṟṟuḷ yāṇṭuṅ kāṇappaṭavilli. pērāciriyar sṭīveṉsaṉ kaṇṭa cila oppumikaḷ aruvaḻakkāyeṅkō mūliyilirun teṭukkappaṭṭa coṟkaḷ  ākum. vēṟu mutaṟcoṟkaḷum, ilakkaṇa amiti oppumikaḷum illāviṭattu ac ciṟupāṉmiyāṉa oppumikaḷik koṇṭu oru muṭivukku varamuṭiyātu. ākavē, mutaṉmiyāṉa vērc coṟkaḷip poṟutta variyil tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukkum, vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷukkumiṭiyē ettuṇi vēṟupāṭu! āṉāl tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ ellā vaṟṟuḷḷum ettuṇi oṟṟumi! viyakkattakka iv vuṇmikaḷik kīḻvarum aṭṭavaṇiyiṟ kāṇalām; taṉmi muṉṉili yorumip peyar, iṭap peyarkaḷuḷ eḻuvāyil ōr uruvum, piṟa vēṟṟumikaḷil vēṟu uruvum, viṉi vikutikaḷil maṟṟōruruvumāka māṟi varuvatuṭaṉ, orē iṭattilum urukkaḷ paṇṭik kālattil oruvakiyākavum, piṟkālattil vēṟākavum  irukkiṉṟamiyāl vēṇṭiya iṭaṅkaḷil avi yāvum  oppumiyākat tarappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
(piṉvarum aṭṭavaṇikaḷil eḷitil oppiṭṭaṟitaṟkāka vaṭamoḻi iṭappeyarkaḷum, turukkiya iṭappeyarkaḷum piṟikkuṟikaḷiṭṭu mutaliṟ koṭukkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.)

kura moḻikaḷiṉ iṭap peyarkaḷukkum tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ iṭap peyarkaḷukkum malikkum maṭuvukkumuḷḷa vēṟṟumiyiruppati nōkka, `am moḻikaḷiṉ tirivukku mūlakāraṇamākat tākkutalkaḷ vēṟeviyāyiṉum ākakkūṭum, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ ākā, eṉpatuteṟṟeṉap pulappaṭukiṉṟatu. āṉāl, atē iṭap peyarkaḷiṉ ārāycci yālēyē pala vaṭamoḻikaḷilum cittiya vikutiyākiya ṉakaram iruppatu kāṇalām. ataṉōṭu, taṉmiyil vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷ oṉṟilēṉum vaṭamoḻi eḻuvāyuru illāmal, turukkiya moḻiyiṉ eḻuvāyip pōṉṟa mi(ṉ) allatu ma(ṉ) eṉṟa cittiya tōṟṟa muṭiya uruvē nilavutal kāṇka. oruvēḷi etirkāla ārāyccikaḷāl tirāviṭa uṟavu uṇṭeṉṟu ēṟpaṭṭālum ēṟpaṭalām. eṉavē, vaṭintiya moḻikaḷiṉ vaṭamoḻic cārpaṟṟa pakutiyum, teṉ intiya moḻikaḷum iraṇṭum āriyac cārpaṟṟavi eṉpatum, iraṇṭum cittiyac cārpuṭiyavi eṉpatun tavira vēṟu evvakiyilum oppumiyuṭiyaṉa alla eṉṟē tuṇiyattakum.
iṉi, ṭākṭar kuṇṭerṭ vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷil maṭṭumalla, vaṭa moḻiyilēkūṭa, tirāviṭat tākkuk kāṇap paṭukiṟatu eṉkiṟār9. inti moḻiyi ārāynta kirus eṉpavarō, vaṭamoḻic cārpaṟṟa pakuti yeṉṟoṉṟu avvaḷavā yiruntirukka vaḻiyilli eṉṟu kūṟi, ataṟkuk kīḻvarum kāraṇaṅkaḷik kāṭṭukiṟār: (1) ammoḻiyiṉ paḻamiyāṉa ilakkaṇa nūlār ataṉik kuṟippiṭātatu. (2) vaṭamoḻic cārpaṟṟateṉa virintu tuṇiyappaṭṭa coṟkaḷ, nuṇṇiya ārāycciyāl, vaṭamoḻi yeṉṟu perumpālum niṟuvappaṭṭu varukiṉṟami. (3) vaṭamoḻi vaḷarcciyuṟṟa kālattil, piṟamoḻit tākkutalkaḷāl vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷ tiripuṟṟamikku eṉṉeṉṉa niyatikaḷ kāṇappaṭṭaṉavō an niyatikaḷi ikkāla vaṭanāṭṭu moḻikaḷum piṉpaṟṟi varukiṉṟami.
icceytiyik kuṟittu 1872il pīms eṉpār “āriyac cārpaṟṟa ikkāla intiya moḻikaḷiṉ oppilakkaṇam”10 eṉṟa nūlil piṉvarumāṟu viḷakkiyuḷḷār: “iṉi, vaṭamoḻiyu mallāta āriyamumallāta coṟkaḷip paṟṟi ārāyvōm. āriyar intiyāvukku vantapōtu tamakkumuṉ aṅkuk kūṭiyirunta makkaḷōṭu neṭunāḷ pōriṭṭu nāṭṭi avarkaḷiṭamiruntu paṟittāka vēṇṭiyiruntatu. attakiya pōrkaḷukkiṭiyē amiti nilaviya kālamum iruntiruttal vēṇṭum. appōtu iru tiṟattiṉarum kalantu oruvaruṭaṉ oruvar karuvippōr ceyyātu, vāḻkkip pōr naṭāttiyiruppar. āriyarallātā riṭiyē āriyaratu tākkē mikutiyāyiruntiruppiṉum, āriyariṭiyē āriyarallātār tākkam oraḷaviṟku illā tirukka muṭiyātu. ākavē, vaṭamoḻiyilēyē āriyac cārpaṟṟaṉavākat tōṉṟum coṟkaḷ cila kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉami iyalpē. ik kāla vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷilō ic coṟkaḷ mikutiyākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē, ec coṟkaḷ āriyac coṟkaḷākat tōṟṟavilliyō aviyellām āriyac cārpaṟṟaviyē eṉṟu kūṟa muṉvaruvatu vaḻakkamāki viṭṭatu.
“iṅṅaṉam āriyarkaḷ paḻaṅ kuṭikaḷiṭamiruntu kaṭaṉ vāṅkiṉar eṉṟa koḷkiyi oppukkoḷvataṟkut taṭikaḷ pala uḷḷaṉa. moḻiyiyaliṟ piṟa cāṉṟukaḷiṉ tuṇiyillāta viṭattu veṟum colloppumi koṇṭu etiyum tuṇintuviṭa muṭiyātu. pala vaḻikaḷilum ciṟanta pēraṟiñarkūṭa it tuṟiyil, “āṉikkum nāṉku kāl, pūṉikkum nāṉku kāl; āṉik kālilum pūṉik kālilum nakaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa” eṉṟa oppumikaḷiṉ pēril muṭivukaṭṭiviṭukiṉṟaṉar. oru tamiḻc collil oru pakaram irukkiṟatu; oru vaṭacollilum oru pakaram irukkiṟatu. ākavē, vaṭacol tamiḻc colliliruntu vantatu eṉṟu kūṟiviṭuvatā! mutalāvatāka, intiyap paḻaṅkuṭikaḷiviṭa uṭalvaliyilum aṟivuvaṉmiyilum āriyarkaḷ uyarvuṭiyavarkaḷākavē iruntaṉar. eṉavē, avarkaḷ ēṟṟativiṭa īntatē mikutiyā yiruntirukka vēṇṭum. iraṇṭāvatutāka, intiyāvukku varuvataṉ muṉṉarē āriyarkaḷ colvaḷa mikkatoru moḻiyi uṭiyavarāyiruntaṉar. eṉavē, uṭi, karuvikaḷ, taṭṭumuṭṭukaḷ, kaṉṟu kālikaḷ, uṭaṟ kūṟukaḷ, uṟavu muṟikaḷ pōṉṟa potuk karuttukkaḷukkuriya coṟkaḷikkūṭa avarkaḷ kaṭaṉvāṅka vēṇṭum nilimi avarkaḷiṭam iruntirukkamuṭiyātu. avarkaḷ kaṭaṉ vāṅkavēṇṭu meṉṟāl putiya ceṭi koṭikaḷ, uyir vakikaḷ, tām muṉṉark kaṇṭirāta putup poruḷkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟiṉ peyarkaḷiyē yākum. ivaṟṟilkūṭat tam moḻic coṟkaḷiyē tirittum kūṭṭiyum putuc coṟkaḷi ākkik koḷḷum āṟṟal ellā moḻikaḷukkum potuvāya amintuḷḷatu. mūṉṟāvatāka, nila iyalip11 poṟuttu eḻum cikkalkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. āriyarvarukiyiṉpōtu avarkaḷuṭaṉ naṭpākavō pākiyākavō uṟavupūṇṭa makkaḷ yār yār? vantēṟiya āriyarka ḷellōrumē tirāviṭarkaḷuṭaṉ neruṅki uṟavāṭi irukkamuṭiyumā? muṭiyumeṉṟāl eppoḻutu? eppaṭi? iṉṟiya ārāycci niliyil iv viṉākkaḷukkut tiṟampaṭa viṭiyiṟuppatu aritu. eṉiṉum, avaṟṟiṟku ētāvatu oru vaḻi kāṇal vēṇṭum. kaṅkik kariyiṉiṉṟum āriyarāl teṟkē viraṭṭappaṭṭa makkaḷ kōlēriyarum, vaṭakkē viraṭṭap paṭṭavar tipēttuk kalappumakkaḷum āvar. eṉavē, kōlēriyar moḻic cāṉṟiṉṟi vaṭamoḻic col oṉṟu nērākat tamiḻiliruntu peṟappaṭṭu viṭṭatu eṉpati oppuk koḷḷutal cālātu. ittakiya nilimiyil ac colliṉ mūlatti ārāyum pakuti uṇmiyil vaṭamoḻiyum, ataṉ kiḷimoḻikaḷum eṉṟa aḷavukkē kuṟukiviṭu meṉpataṟkiya milli. āḻntu nōkkiṉāl atil perumpālum ataṟkuriya vērccollik kāṇalām.”
ik kūṟṟu oruvāṟu oppukkoḷḷak kūṭiyatēyām. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷip paṟṟiyavari vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷil avaṟṟiṉ tākkutal oruciṟitutāṉ iruntirukkak kūṭum. āṉāl, cittiyat tākkutalkaḷālō aṉṟi āriyac cārpaṟṟa piṟa moḻikaḷiṉ tākkutalkaḷālō, vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷ iṉṟiya tirinta niliyi aṭintirukkak kūṭāvō eṉpatu vēṟu kēḷvi. tirāviṭam, cikattiyam, āriyac cārpaṟṟavi eṉpaṉa oru poruṭ kiḷavikaḷalla. pīms eṉpār `uyir olimāṟṟaṅkaḷ’12 eṉṟa pirivil, “āriya uyirolikaḷ intiyāvukku vaṭakkilum kiḻakkilum naṭuvilum teṟkilum uḷḷa veḷi iṉattārāl evvaḷavu māṟutalaṭintaṉa veṉṟu collamuṭiyātu. āṉāl, māṟutal aṭintuḷḷaṉa eṉpatu maṭṭum maṟukkamuṭiyāta uṇmiyākum” eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār. vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷuḷ akaramum ākāramum ēkāramāka māṟiyami, ittakiya āriyac cārpaṟṟa  māṟutal eṉṟu avar kūṟukiṟār. nāvaṭi aṇṇa meyyākiya ḷakarattip paṟṟik kūṟukiyil, atu rakara ṭakaraṅkaḷuṭaṉ mayaṅkukiṉṟatu eṉṟum, atu naṭu intiyāviluḷḷa kōlar kaḷiṉ kuḻuvil kāṇappaṭukiṟatu eṉṟum avar urikkiṉṟār. ik kōlarkaḷuṭaṉum tirāviṭarkaḷuṭaṉum neṭunāḷ uṟavu koṇṭirunta marāṭṭiya, oriyā makkaḷiṭi, inta ḷakaram mikutiyākap payilvatu viyappaṉṟu eṉṟum avar kūṟukiṟār.
“cinti moḻiyiṉ ilakkaṇam”13 eṉṟa nūlil ṭākṭar erṉasṭu ṭrampu āriyac cārpaṟṟa allatu tāttāriya moḻiyiṉat tākkutalkaḷ vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa’ eṉṟu uṟutiyākak kūṟukiṟār; muṉ nā aṇpal oliyākiya takara nakara sakaraṅkaḷiṉiṭamāka, avi nāaṭi aṇṇa oliyākiya ṭakara ṇakara ṣakaraṅkaḷip peritum ēṟkiṉṟami ataṟkuc cāṉṟu pakarum eṉṟum kūṟukiṟār. atē pōṉṟu uyirppu oliyākiya ha eṉpatip pirākiruta moḻi vilakkuvatu nōkka, “atip pēciya potu makkaḷiṭiyē tāttāriyat tākku iruntirukkavēṇṭum. teṟkēyuḷḷa tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil iv eḻuttu illāmi kāṇka” eṉṟum kūṟukiṟār. mēlum, tirāviṭa moḻiyiṉattic cērnta teluṅku `cca’, `ja’ eṉṟa eḻuttukkaḷi `ṭca’ eṉṟum, ṭsa eṉṟum olippatu pōlavē marāṭṭi moḻiyum olikkiṉṟatu; ātalāl tirāviṭat tākku marāṭṭiyiluḷḷatu veḷippaṭi eṉṟum avar muṭivukaṭṭukiṟār.
aṭikkuṟippukaḷ
1.	cune ṉuca. ḷuvanenaḷēṭin ṭika kṣaṭiayela - tuṭiracayēṭa ṭika vāna haḷayiviu ḷuṭiunivala ṭika kṣaṭiayela..
2.	āca. ḻaṭiṉapaḷaṭin ṭika cūnayīyaṭa - tuṭiracayēṭa ṭika vāna haḷayiviu ḷaṭiunivala ṭika kṣanapēyaṭa; yaṭaḷaṭi “habṭicapiinēḷa ṭika aiṉēyi.”
3.	“kūyavayacanayan ṭica ūhyaṭaṉanana”
4.	ḷuci ṟu. tuṭinēḷa (haḷayiviu cunaḷanayacuhnaḷa ēṭiṭa.i)
5.	aicūṉayin havēiḷūrayacala (hayīcaṭi 1872)
6.	āca. ḻucaṭiṟaḷana kṣa.ū.ḷa.
7.	ṭīḷavayima
8.	ṭuyapērayapana ṭika vāna ṭuyayīyīḷa.
9.	kūhna tuṭiracayēṭa ṭika vāna ḻunacaayan ṭīcanivēyaṭa ḷuṭiunivala kaṭica 1869.
10.	“ha uṭiayīyacayaviena ḻucayaaayaca ṭika vāna āṭiṉanacan cūṭin-hacalayan ṭuyapērayapana ṭika aiṉēyi” le tuṭihn kṣanayaanaḷa kṣa.ū.ḷu. 1872.
11.	ḻunaṭipacayayīhla.
12.	ēṭiṟanaṭa uhyapēnaḷa.
13.	“ḻucayaaayaca ṭika vāna ḷiṉōi ṭuyapērayapana” - ṉuca. nucanēḷava kūcaraayīyī.

7. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ enta iṉattic cērntavi?
tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ cittiya moḻiyiṉattōṭu cērttaṟkuriyaṉavē yaṉṟi intu - airōppiya moḻiyiṉat tōṭu cērttaṟkuriyaṉavākā eṉpatu pērāciriyar rāsk1 eṉpāriṉ muṭipākum. cērttaṟkuriyaṉa eṉṟu maṭṭum kūṟalāmē yaṉṟi, cērntaṉa eṉṟu colliviṭutaṟkilli; avi cittiya moḻikaḷōṭu toṭarpuṭiyaṉa vēyaṉṟic cittiya moḻiyiṉattiliruntu peṟappaṭṭaviyalla. cittiya moḻiyīṉam eṉpatil piṉṉiṣ, turukki, maṅkōliyam, tuṅkūsiyam ākiyamoḻiyiṉaṅkaḷ aṭaṅkum. rāsk eṉpārē ivvāṟu mutaṉmutal vakippaṭuttiṉār. im moḻikaḷ aṉittum orē ilakkaṇa amippuṭi yaviyāy, orē paṭittāṉa potu vitikaḷiyum piṉpaṟṟu kiṉṟaṉa. urupukaḷ allatu tuṇic coṟkaḷic cērppataṉālēyē avi ilakkaṇa vēṟupāṭuṟukiṉṟaṉa; cemittiya2 moḻiyiṉaṅ kaḷilō pakutiyiṉ uyirolikaḷi māṟṟuvatāl vēṟupāṭuṟu kiṉṟaṉa; cīṉattilum, piṟa taṉippaṭṭa ōraci moḻikaḷilum coṟṟoṭariluḷḷa coṟkaḷi muṉṉum piṉṉum māṟṟi yamippatāl vēṟupaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivvakiyil intu - airōppiya moḻikaḷum cittiya moḻikaḷip pōlavē mutaṉmutal aṭukkiyal3 muṟiyiyē kiyāṇṭiruntaṉa eṉṟu tōṟṟu kiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl avaṟṟiṉ aṭikaḷ allatu tuṇic coṟkaḷvaravarat tēyntu vikutikaḷākavō urupukaḷākavō māṟiviṭṭamiyāl avi cittiya iṉaṅkaḷip pōlavumiṉṟi, cīṉa moḻikaḷip pōlavumiṉṟit taṉippaṭṭa oruvaki iṉamāyiṉa. cittiya moḻikaḷic cilar tāttār moḻiyiṉam eṉpar; piṉṉiṣ iṉam, āltāyik iṉam, maṅkōliya iṉam, turāṉiya iṉam eṉṟum palavāṟākap palar kūṟuvar. āṉāl, iviyaṉittum ipperu moḻik kuḻuviluḷḷa moḻi yiṉaṅkaḷ palavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟiraṇṭiyē taṉittaṉikkuṟikkumāta lāl avaṟṟip peru moḻik kuḻuviṉ peyarākak koḷḷal cālātu. itukāṟum, paṇṭik kirēkka aṟiñarkaḷāl cittiyam eṉṟa peyar potuppaṭa āciyāvilum, airōppā vilum iruntuvanta nākarikamaṟṟa paṇṭi makkaḷik kuṟikkavē payaṉpaṭuttap peṟṟuvantuḷḷamiyāl ataṉip peyarāka vaḻaṅkutalē cālpuṭittām.
pēraṟiñar rāsk tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ cittiyac cārpuṭi yavi eṉak kūṟiṉavarkaḷuḷ mutalvarāyiṉum, ataṉit teḷivupaṭutti viḷakka ettakiya muyaṟciyum avar ceyya villi. avarip piṉpaṟṟiya palarum atēmātiri avar kūṟiyatik kūṟiṉarēyaṉṟi vēṟu oṉṟum ceyya villi. piriccārṭu4 eṉpār tam ārāyccikaḷil cittiya tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil cila potuppaṭiyāṉa oṟṟumikaḷi oṉṟiraṇṭu ilakkaṇac cārpāṉa eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷuṭaṉ viḷakkip pōyiṉar. ātāraṅkaḷuṭaṉ āyntu viḷakkuvatu eḷitaṉṟu. ēṉeṉil aṅṅaṉam viḷakka vēṇṭumāyiṉ mutal mutalil tirāviṭa moḻikaḷit taṉittaṉi ārāyntu avaṟṟiṉ oppumiyiṉmūlam avaṟṟiṉ paṇṭiya urukkaḷiyum ilakkaṇa amitikaḷiyum talimiyāṉa paṇpukaḷiyum aṟivatōṭu, atēmātiri piṟa cittiya uṭpirivukaḷiṉ paṇpu kaḷiyum uṇarntāka vēṇṭum. “ulaka varalāṟṟiṉ potup paṭiyāṉa amitikaḷ” eṉṟa tokutiyil māksmūlaratu “turāṉiyamoḻikaḷ paṟṟiya namatu iṉṟiya aṟivunili”5 eṉṟa ārāycciyuri piṉṉar veḷivantatu. an nūlmikac ciṟanta toṉṟu. eṉiṉum, annūlil āciriyar tām eṭuttukkoṇṭa poruḷ muḻuvatiyum, potuppaṭavē ārāyntuḷḷār. ataṉāl īṇṭu eṭuttukkoṇṭa poruḷukku atu putuc cāṉṟukaḷ oṉṟiyum pakaravilli. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇa amitikaḷum, coṟṟoṭar muṟiyum mēṟpōkkākap pārppavarkkukkūṭa viḷaṅkak kūṭumāyiṉum, avaṟṟiṉ olimuṟi, avaṟṟiṉ iṭavaki māṟṟaṅkaḷ, vaḻakkukaḷ, ilakkaṇa urumāṟṟaṅkaḷ mutaliyavi taṉippaṭṭa ārāycci yālaṉṟi viḷaṅkat takkaviyalla.
iṉi, ivvaki ārāycciyit toṭaṅkumuṉ pōṭliṅ6 eṉpār variyaṟuttuk kūṟiya, piṉvarum karuttu niṉivu kūraṟpāṟṟu: “tām naṉku ārāyntaṟiyāta moḻi kaḷippaṟṟi oruvar eḻuta muṟpaṭutal tīṅku viḷippatākum”. cittiya moḻiyippaṟṟi ārāyavēṇṭumāṉāl, avaṟṟu ḷaṭaṅkiya palvēṟu moḻikaḷiṉ oppiyalkaḷum vēṟṟumi yiyalkaḷum naṉku terintiruttal vēṇṭuma. pālar7 eṉpār eḻutiya piṉṉiya moḻiyārāyccik kaṭṭurikaḷum, pērāciriyar haṉ/palvi8 eṉpār eḻutiya turāṉiya moḻi yārāyccikaḷum ittuṟiyil pērutavi puriya vallaṉavām. haṅkēriyaṉ, vōkul9 ostiyāk, piṉṉiṣ ākiya nāṉku moḻikaḷum orē potu moḻiyiṉattic cōntavi eṉṟum, avaṟṟiṉ ilakkaṇaṅkaḷellām neruṅkiya toṭarpuṭiyaṉa eṉṟum haṉ/palvi viḷakkiyuḷḷār.
pēraṟiñar pāṭ, piraṭerik mullar10 mutaliya pala moḻi yiyalār cittiya allatu turāṉiya moḻikaḷ orupotu moḻiyiliruntu vantavi eṉṟu uṟutiyākak kūṟamuṭiyātu eṉṟu karutukiṉṟaṉar. pēraṟiñar piḷākkum11 “turāṉiya moḻikaḷ palavum orupuṭi orē paṭittāṉa vaḷarcciyi aṭintuḷḷa orē kuḻu moḻikaḷō allatu pala kuḻu moḻikaḷō ākum” eṉak karutukiṉṟaṉar. eṉiṉum potuppaṭa nōkka, kastrēṉ12 mutaliya aṟiñar kūṟukiṉṟapaṭi avaṟṟiṉ oṟṟumikaḷ moḻikku iṉṟiyamiyātaviyākavum, palavākavum irukkiṟapaṭiyāl avaṟṟāl ēṟpaṭum toṭakkap potumiyi, a/tāvatu orē mutaṉmoḻiyiṉiṉṟu avi toṭaṅki yirukkavēṇṭum eṉṟa muṭivi, ēṟkātirukka muṭiyātu. “im moḻikaḷiṉ col oṟṟumikaḷum vaṭiva oṟṟumikaḷum āriya cemittiya iṉaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum oṟṟumiyiṉiṉṟum vēṟupaṭṭavi yāyiṉum, taṟceyalāy ēṟpaṭak kūṭiyavi alla, orē mutaṉmoḻiyiliruntu ēṟpaṭṭaviyākavē irukkavēṇṭum eṉpatu teḷivu” eṉpar aṟiñar māksmūlarum, mēlum “neṭunāḷ pirintiyaṅkiya aṭukkiyal moḻikaḷuḷ, moḻiyiyaṟkiyiṉ aṭippaṭip pakutikaḷ allatu māṟupāṭu ceyyamuṭiyāta col vakikaḷāṉa iṭappeyarkaḷ, eṇṇup peyarkaḷ, urupukaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟit tavira vēṟu ettakiya oṟṟumikaḷiyum nām etirpārkka muṭiyātu. mēṟpaṭiyāṉa poruḷ koṇṭa coṟkaḷuḷ vēṟṟumi iruppativiṭa, oṟṟumi yiruppatit tāṉ nām arumiyāyk koḷḷa vēṇṭum” eṉṟum avar kūṟukiṟār.
mēlē tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ vaṭamoḻiyiṉiṉṟum vēṟupaṭṭavi eṉpataṟkuk kāṭṭiya ak kāraṇaṅkaḷiyē avi cittiya iṉaṅkaḷuṭaṉ oṉṟupaṭṭavi eṉpataṟkum kāṭṭalākum. cittiya iṉattuṭpaṭṭa moḻikaḷ aṉittum orē iṉam eṉpatiyum ak kāraṇaṅkaḷē kāṭṭum. avaṟṟiṉ viḷakkattik kuṟinta aḷavil eṭuttuk koṇṭāluṅkūṭa avi toṭarpu uṭiyavi eṉpatu aiyamaṟat teḷivākum. cila kūṟukaḷil maṭṭum tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ intu - airōppiya moḻikaḷi oṭṭiyaviyāyk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa; a/tāvatu ōraḷavukku, “aṭukkiyal” moḻiyāy iruppatiliruntu muṉṉēṟi (intu-airōppiya moḻikaḷip pōla)c “citiviyal”13 moḻikaḷ ākiyuḷḷaṉa. vēṟṟumiyilum viṉittiripilum payaṉpaṭum tuṇic coṟkaḷiṟ pala taṉi moḻiyāka vaḻakkāṟaṟṟuppōyiṉa. āṉāl, i/toṉṟi muṉṉiṭṭu, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷic cittiya iṉattiliruntu pirikka vēṇṭiyatilli; ēṉeṉil it tuṇiccoṟkaḷ ciṟcila iṭaṅkaḷil muṟṟilum urupukaḷ allatu vikutikaḷākak kuṟintuviṭṭaṉavāyiṉum, coṟkaḷiṉ pakutikaḷiliruntu ivi eppoḻutum pirikkappaṭak kūṭiyaṉavākavē iruntu varukiṉṟaṉa; intu-airōppiya moḻikaḷiṟ pōṉṟu ivi pakutiyuṭaṉ oṉṟupaṭṭu orē moḻiyiṉ kūṟāyviṭavilli. intu - airōppiya moḻikaḷil ivi aṅṅaṉam kalantu viṭṭapaṭiyāl pakuti etu, vikuti etu eṉṟu pirippatu kūṭa aritāy viṭukiṟatu. ataṉōṭu, turukkiyam, piṉṉiyam, haṅkēriyam, jappāṉiyam mutaliya moḻikaḷ aṉittum tirāviṭa moḻiyiviṭac “citiviyal” tuṟiyil mēmpaṭṭiruppiṉum, akkāraṇattāl cittiya iṉattiliruntu vilakkappaṭavilli eṉpatiyum ōrtal vēṇṭum.
“moḻiyiyalil cīṉattiṉ iṭam” eṉṟa nūlil eṭkiṉs cittiya iṉattiṉ orumi, avaṟṟuṭaṉ tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ toṭarpu ākiya koḷkikaḷip peritum ēṟṟuk koḷḷukiṉṟār. avar kāṭṭum pala taṟceyalāṉa, nuṇukka māṉa oṟṟumikaḷ āḻntu ārāyumaḷavil niliniṟkā. āyiṉum potuppaṭak kūṟumaḷavil ittuṟiyil oruvar ārāyuntōṟum tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ maṅkōliya moḻikaḷ ākiya iraṇṭum oruvāṟu orumippāṭuṭiyaṉa eṉpatik kāṇpar. tirāviṭa cīṉa moḻikaḷiṭi oṟṟumikaḷ mikuti eṉak kūṟamuṭiyātu. eṭkiṉs “cittiya iṉammaṭṭumaṉṟu, āciya airōppiya moḻikaḷ aṉittumē orē mutaṉmoḻiyiṉ vakit tiripukaḷ ākum” eṉkiṟār. ituvari maṉita vakuppiṉ moḻikaḷi aṟiñar ārāynta aḷavil ikkōṭpāṭu poruttamākat tōṟṟiṉum aṟiviyal muṟippaṭi itu muṟṟilum nilināṭṭappaṭa iṉṉum neṭunāḷ cellak kūṭum. kaṭiciyāka, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ amippukaḷi viḷakkac cittiya moḻiyiṉatti oṟṟumippaṭutti ārāynta aṟivu evvaḷavō payaṉpaṭukiṟatu eṉṟa oru ceytiyē avaṟṟuḷ cittiyat toṭarpu irukkiṟatu eṉpataṟkōr aṟikuṟi yākum. itaṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭākat tirāviṭa moḻiyiluḷḷa viṉiccoṟkaḷiṉ peyareccattiṉ amippi eṭuttuk koḷḷalām. maṅkōliyam, mañcu ivaṟṟil i/tu eṅṅaṉam amintuḷḷatu eṉpati aṟinta piṉṉarē, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil eṅṅaṉam amintuḷḷatu eṉpati eḷitil aṟiyamuṭintatu. aṅṅaṉam aṟinta viḷakkatti ituvari yārum maṟukkavō allatu vēṟu vakiyil viḷakkavō illi eṉpatu kuṟipiṭattakkatu.
cittiya iṉaccārpuk koḷkiyi uṟutippaṭuttum vakiyil maṟṟoru cāṉṟuṅ kiṭittuḷḷatu. atuvē mēṟku muṭiyā14 allatu pārasīkattil uḷḷa pehistaṉ allatu pakistāṉ aṭṭavaṇikaḷiṉ moḻipeyarppukkaḷ ākum. iv aṭṭavaṇikaḷ ṭariyas hisṭāspas15 eṉpavar tammippaṟṟit tāmē eḻutiya varalāṟu ākum. ivi paḻam pārasīka moḻiyilum, pāpilōṉiya moḻiyilum, pārasīka, mīṭiyap pēraraciluḷḷa cittiya makkaḷum aṟiyum paṭiyāka avarkaḷatu tāymoḻiyilum eḻutappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ moḻipeyarppu tirāviṭa cittiya uṟavi viḷakkum vakiyil oru puttoḷiyāy viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. ivaṟṟiṉ iraṇṭām tokuti teḷivākac cittiyac cārpāṉatu eṉṟu nāris16 tamatu kaṭṭuriyil eḻutukiṟār.17 pēraṟiñar āpparṭ18 im moḻiyip pēciya makkaḷ mīṭiyarē eṉṟu kūṟukiṟār. āyiṉum avarum im moḻi cittiya allatu turāṉiyac cārpāṉateṉṟē koḷkiṟār. itaṉāl, ki.mu. 5ām nūṟṟāṇṭilēyē pēcappaṭṭa oru cittiya iṉa moḻiyuṭaṉ tirāviṭa moḻikaḷi oṟṟumippaṭuttum ōr ariya vāyppuk kiṭittuḷḷatu. immoḻi potuppaṭac cittiya iṉac cārpuṭiyatē eṉiṉum, avaṟṟuṭ ciṟappāka ukrō - piṉṉiṣ kuḻuvuṭaṉ iṉṉum neruṅkiya toṭarpuṭiya tākavuṅ kāṭṭappaṭukiṟatu. tirāviṭamoḻikaḷ inta ukrō - piṉṉiṣ moḻikaḷic ciṟappāka ottirukkiṉṟaṉa eṉpatu iṅkē kūṟavēṇṭiya toṉṟākum. 
tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukkum, ip pehistaṉ aṭṭa vaṇiyiṉ moḻikkum iṭiyē kāṇappaṭum oṟṟumikaḷ varumāṟu:
(1)  iv aṭṭavaṇikaḷil ṭ ṭa, ṭa, ṇa eṉṟa nā aṭi aṇṇa olikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. itē olikaḷ tirāviṭa moḻikaḷilum uḷḷaṉa. vaṭamoḻiyilum ivi uḷḷaṉa vāyiṉum (piṟa āriya moḻikaḷuḷ illāmiyi nōkkat) tirāviṭa moḻikaḷip piṉpaṟṟi vaṭamoḻiyil ēṟpaṭṭa olikaḷē ivi eṉa ūkikkalām. nārisum ik karuttiyē veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷār.
(2) tirāviṭa moḻikaḷuḷ tamiḻippōla, iv aṭṭavaṇi moḻikaḷuḷ valleḻuttukkaḷ moḻimutalil varumpoḻutu iraṭṭitta iṭattum valloli uṭiyavi yākavum, moḻi iṭiyē uyirkaḷiṉ naṭuvē varum poḻutu aṇmi allatu ari aṅkāppu oliyuṭiyavākavum olikkiṉṟaṉa.
(3) ivvaṭṭavaṇi moḻiyiṉ āṟām vēṟṟumi urupukaḷ ṉa, ṉiṉa, iṉṉa eṉpaviyākum. ivaṟṟiṟkuc cariyāṉa tirāviṭa urupukaḷ teluṅku ṉī eṉpatum, kōṇṭu allatu pirākuvi ṉa a eṉpaviyum, tamiḻ iṉ eṉpatum ākum.
(4) aṭṭavaṇikaḷil nāṉkām vēṟṟumi urupu ikki allatu ikka eṉpatākum. itaṉi yotta urupukaḷ tāttār - turukkiyak kuḻuvilum, ukriyaṉ iṉattilum uḷa. āṉāl itaṉi muṟṟilum ottiruppavi tirāviṭa urupukaḷākiya ku, ki, ka eṉpaviyām. tamiḻ, maliyāḷa moḻikaḷil ivvurupukaḷ iraṭṭikkumpōtu avaṟṟiṉ muṉ u allatu i collolic cāriyiyāka varukiṉṟatu. aṭṭavaṇikaḷil uṉakku eṉṉum poruḷil niṉikka (niṉ-ikka) eṉṉum colluṭaṉ kaṉṉaṭam niṉake (niṉ-a-ke) maliyāḷam niṉakku (niṉ=a-k) eṉpavaṟṟi oppiṭṭu nōkkuka.
(5) aṭṭavaṇikaḷiluḷḷa iṭappeyarkaḷiṉ iraṇṭām vēṟṟumi urupu uṉ, iṉ, ṉ eṉpaviyām. ittuṭaṉ teluṅku uruvu ṉu, ṉi eṉpatiyum kaṉṉaṭam am, aṉṉu (aṉṉ- u) eṉpatiyum oppiṭṭu nōkkuka.
(6) aṭṭavaṇikaḷil varum eṇṇup peyarkaḷil eḻuttil eḻutiya peyar oṉṟu eṉṉum poruḷil varum kir eṉṟa col maṭṭumēyām. i/tu uṇmiyil peyaruriccol vaṭivattilēyē (a/tāvatu paṇput tokiyākavē) eḻutappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. teluṅkil ivvaṭivam oka- eṉpatum, tamiḻil ōr -eṉpatum ākum. ku-moḻiyil tamiḻ oru eṉpataṟkuc cariyāṉa col ra eṉpatām. ik ku moḻiyiṉ vaṭivattiṟku aṭṭavaṇiyiluḷḷa piṟitoru vaṭivamākiya ra allatu irra mikavum nerukkiyatākum.
aṭṭavaṇiyil eṇṇup peyarkaḷ peyaraṭiyākum poḻutu im eṉṟa cāriyik koḷḷukiṉṟaṉa. itu tamiḻ ām eṉpatiyum sāmōyiyat im eṉpatiyum niṉivūṭṭu kiṉṟatu.
(7) tirāviṭa moḻikaḷilum, aṭṭavaṇiyilum muṉṉilip peyar oṉṟē. ivi iraṇṭilum eḻuvāy nī; piṟavēṟṟamikku muṉṉiya mutal vaṭivu niṉ eṉpatākum. tamakkuk kīḻppaṭiyiluḷḷavarkaḷi aḻikka muṉṉili orumiyiyē vaḻaṅkivanta kāraṇattāl ivvaṭṭa vaṇikaḷil muṉṉilippaṉmi kāṇappaṭavilli.
(8) aṭṭavaṇi moḻiyilum tirāviṭa moḻikaḷip pōlavē peyareccam irukkiṟatu. āṉāl atōṭu kūṭavē atil iṇippu iṭappeyar19 oṉṟum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. āṉāl ippakuti pāracīkattiṉiṉṟum moḻipeyarkkap paṭṭatātalāl pāracīkattiluḷḷa iṇippu iṭappeyarit tāṉ iṅkē puṉintu uṇṭupaṇṇi yirukkavēṇṭum eṉṟu nāris karutukiṟār. aṭṭavaṇiyiṉ peyareccam uruvil tirāviṭap peyareccattiṉiṉṟum vēṟupaṭṭiruppiṉum ataṉ poruḷum payaṉum tirāviṭa vaḻakki ottē irukkiṉṟaṉa. ippeyareccamē cittiya iṉac cārpuṭiya moḻikaḷ aṉittiṟkum potuvāṉa ciṟappup paṇpu eṉak koḷḷalām pōlum!
(9) aṭṭavaṇiyiṉ ēval etirmaṟi muṭipu iṉṉi kōṇṭil itu miṉṉi ākum.
ivvoṟṟumikaḷ nīṅkalākap piṟavēṟṟumit tiripukaḷil pehistaṉ aṭṭavaṇiyiṉ moḻi haṅkēriyam, mōrṭuviṉ20 mutaliya piṟa ukriya moḻikaḷi ottirukkiṉṟatu. āṉāl, ivvakikaḷilellām tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ vēṟupaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ viṉiccollākkam cikkalaṟṟatu. avi perumpālum kālaṅkāṭṭum iṭinilikaḷuṭaṉum, avaṟṟiṉpiṉ, pāl, iṭam kāṭṭa avaṟṟiṟkuriya iṭappeyar kaḷiṉ tiripukaḷiyē koṇṭum viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. ivvakiyil vēṟṟumikaḷ iruntumkūṭa, mēṟkūṟiya oṟṟumikaḷ iṉṟiyamiyāc ciṟappuṭiyaṉa ātaliṉ, tirāviṭa moḻi kaḷukkum cittiya moḻikaḷukkum iṭiyiluḷḷa uṟavu oruciṟitēyāyiṉum a/tu aṭippaṭiyāṉatē eṉpatil aiyamirukka muṭiyātu. ēṉeṉil, ivvaḷavu vēṟṟumi kaḷukkiṭiyilum iṉṟiyamiyā oṟṟumikaḷum kāṇak kiṭakkiṉṟaṉavātaliṉ. ivvoṟṟumikaḷiliruntu peṟap paṭuvaṉa yāvi yeṉil, tirāviṭar varalāṟṟuk kālaṅkaḷukku neṭunāḷ muṉṉarē innāṭṭilēyē iruntavarkaḷ eṉpatum, avarkaḷum ulaka makkaṭ kuḻuviṉar aṉivarukkum mutaṟ piṟappiṭamākak karutappaṭum naṭu āciyāviṉiṉṟē iṅku vantavarkaḷ eṉpatumēyām. mutalil āriyariṭamiruntum, piṉ ukrō- turāṉiyariṭamiruntum pirintu ivarkaḷ intiyāvi nōkki vantirukkavēṇṭum eṉpatum, varum vaḻiyil palūccistāṉattil oru pakutiyiṉarik kuṭiyēṟaviṭṭu vantirukkavēṇṭum eṉpatum eḷitil ūkikkappaṭum.
iṅṅaṉam ilakkaṇa amippu mutaliya pala oṟṟumip paṇpukaḷiṉ mūlam tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ cittiyac cārpuṭiyavi eṉṟu koḷḷappaṭiṉum, avi intu -  airōppiya moḻiyiṉac cārpum uṭiyaviyē eṉpatu viyakkattakka cila oppumikaḷāl uyttuṇarappaṭum. avaṟṟiṉ cittiyat toṭarpukūṭat taṉippaṭat turukkiyak kuḻuvuṭaṉō, ukriya, maṅkōliya, tuṅkūsiya moḻikaḷuṭaṉō aṉṟu; am moḻikaḷ aṉittum tammuḷ evvaḷavō vēṟṟumi yuṭiyaṉa; āyiṉum avi yaṉittiṟkum, tāyāyuḷḷa oru pēriṉa moḻiyuṭaṉēyē tirāviṭa moḻiyiṉaṅkaḷ toṭarpuṭiyaviyām. ivaṟṟuḷ tirāviṭattiṟku mikavum aṇmiyuṭiyateṉak koḷḷakkūṭiyatu piṉṉiṣ allatu ukriyamē. ostiyakkuṭaṉum cila ciṟappāṉa uṟavukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. ilakkaṇa oppumi, col tokuti oppumi ākiyavaṟṟiṉ mūlamākaṭṭum ārāyntu kaṇṭa im muṭipu pehistaṉ aṭṭavaṇikaḷiṉ veḷiyīṭṭāl peritum uṟutiyaṭivatu kāṇka. itaṉāl mīṭō - pāracīkaratu paṭi yeṭuppiṟku muṉ naṭu āciyāvil kuṭiyirunta makkaḷ turukkiya allatu maṅkōliyak kuḻuviṉar allar, ukriyak kuḻuviṉarē eṉpatu viḷaṅkum. veppa maṇṭalattuḷ irukkum tirāviṭak kuḻu eṅkē, vaṭa turuvap pakkattilirukkum piṉṉiyar, ostiyakkar, ukriyar eṅkē!ivarkaḷiṭiyē ettakiya toṭarpu?
tirāviṭa moḻikaḷic cittiyac cārpuṭiyavi eṉṟu kaṇittaṟkāṉa teḷivukaḷi ārāykiyil niṉivil vikka vēṇṭiya toṉṟuṇṭu. a/tāvatu, orupuṟam cittiya iṉa moḻikaḷiṉ aṭippaṭiyāṉa “tokka iyal” paṇpukaḷ teḷivāṉa vuruvuṭiyavi āka iruppiṉum, iṉṉorupuṟam col tokutiyilum, vēṟu pala ciṟu kuṟippukaḷilum oṉṟukkoṉṟu malikkum maṭuvukkum uḷḷa vēṟṟumi uṭiyaviyā yirukkiṉṟaṉa eṉpatē. uṇmiyil, intu - airōppiya iṉa moḻikaḷ oṉṟukku oṉṟu vēṟṟumip paṭuvativiṭa, cittiya moḻikaḷ oṉṟukkoṉṟu ettaṉiyō maṭaṅku mikunta vēṟṟumiyuṭiyaṉa eṉṟu kūṟutal vēṇṭum. eṭuttuk kāṭṭāka, intu - airōppiya moḻikaḷ aṉittilum eṇkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ ottirukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu coṉṉāṟ pōtātu, (vaṭamoḻi oṉṟu eṉṉum poruḷ koṇṭa ēka eṉpatu nīṅkalāka maṟṟavi aṉittum) orē vaṭivuṭiyavi yākavē irukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟuṅ kūṟutal vēṇṭum. itaṟku nērmāṟāka, cittiya iṉa moḻikaḷil aṉittiṟkum potuvāṉa oṟṟumikaḷ arukiyirukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu coṉṉāṟ pōtātu; ētēṉum iraṇṭu moḻikaḷukkup potuvāṉa eṇkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷkūṭa mika māṟupaṭṭē yirukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟuṅ kūṟutal vēṇṭum. iv vēṟṟumikaḷiṉ mikutippāṭṭi nōkkiṉ, intac cittiya iṉam intu - airōppiya iṉattip pōl oru taṉi iṉam eṉṟu colvativiṭap pala iṉaṅkaḷi uṭkoṇṭatōr pēriṉam eṉṟu colvatē cālum. ippēriṉattuṭpaṭṭa kuḻukkaḷiṉ orumippāṭu nuṇukkamāṉa oṟṟumikaḷāl niṟuvakkūṭiya taṉṟu, perumpōkkāṉa vaṭivu allatu amippu oṟṟumi kaḷiṉālēyē niṟuvappaṭum. eṉavē, intu airōppiya iṉattuṭpaṭṭa moḻikaḷākiya vaṭamoḻi, sentu (kīḻap paḻam pārasīkam), paḻampārasīkam, kirēkkam, ilattīṉam, kātiklittu vēṉiyam, slavōṉiyam, kelttiyam mutaliyavi ovvoṉṟum ōriṉattuṭpaṭṭa taṉi moḻikaḷēyām. āṉāl, cittiyap pēriṉattilē ivaṟṟilum mikunta vēṟṟumikaḷi yuṭiya ciṟṟiṉa moḻikaḷ aintāṟukku mēṟpaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivvuṭpiriviṉaṅkaḷ ovvoṉṟilum kiṭṭattaṭṭa intu - airōppiya moḻikaḷil ettaṉit taṉi moḻikaḷ uṇṭō attaṉit taṉi moḻikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. ivipōka vakippaṭutti innāḷvari iṉamākac cērkka muṭiyāta niliyuṭiya irupatu muppatu taṉi moḻikaḷkūṭa uḷḷaṉa.
intu - airōppiya iṉattiṟkum cittiyap pēriṉattiṟkum iṭiyil uḷḷa ipperu vēṟṟumikkuk kāraṇam mutaṉmutaṟ koṇṭē muṉṉiya iṉattavar aṟivum nākarikamum paṭittiruntamiyum, avarkaḷ moḻikaḷ iṉṉum muntiya nāḷ mutaṟkoṇṭē tirutta muṟṟu mutaṟcoṟkaḷum amitikaḷum citiyāmal pātukākkappaṭṭu vantuḷḷamiyum tāṉ. cittiya iṉattavar nāṭōṭikaḷāyt tiripavarā yiruntamiyum, āriyar oḻuṅkāṉa kuṭivāḻkki uṭiyavarā yiruntamiyum itaṟku aṭippaṭit tūṇṭutalkaḷā yiruntirukka vēṇṭum. kāraṇam etuvāyiṉum cari, cittiya moḻikaḷiṉ iṉa uṟavukaḷi ārāyum vakiyil icceytiyi niṉivil vittuk koḷḷal vēṇṭum. neṭunāḷ pirikkappaṭṭa cittiya iṉa moḻikaḷiṭiyē atē iyalpuṭiya intu - airōppiya moḻikaḷil kāṇappaṭum nuṇukkamāṉa oṟṟumikaḷi etirpārkka muṭiyātu. tiruntā moḻikaḷiṭiyē, oru moḻiyiṉ vakittiripukaḷiṭik kūṭa mika virivāṉa māṟutalkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭu, māṟupāṭum mikutiyāyviṭṭataṟkāṉa eṭuttuk kāṭṭukaḷip pēraṟiñar māksmūlar tamatu “moḻiyiyal coṟpoḻivukaḷil” kuṟippiṭukiṟār. itē māksmūlarukku varinta kaṭitamoṉṟil pāṭṭisaṉ pātiriyārum “oṉṟaṉi yoṉṟu aṭuttuḷḷa tīvukaḷiraṇṭiṉ moḻikaḷ col tokutiyil muṟṟilum vēṟupaṭṭum, amippu oṉṟil maṭṭumē oṉṟupaṭṭum kāṇappaṭuvatu iyalpu” eṉṟu itaṉōṭoppak kūṟutal kāṇka.
oru taṉimoḻikku oru moḻiyiṉattuṭaṉ mūṉṟu vakiyāṉa uṟavumuṟi irukka muṭiyum. (1) nērāṉa kālvaḻi muṟi (2) uṭaṉiyaṅkun tuṇimoḻi muṟi (3) avviṉattuṭ cārum cārpumuṟi. ivaṟṟuḷ mūṉṟām uṟavu muṟiyiyē tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ cittiya moḻiyiṉattuṭaṉ koṇṭuḷḷaṉavākak kūṟalām. eṉiṉum, tirāviṭa moḻiyārāycciyāḷarkaḷ itaṉi oppukkoṇṭatākak kūṟa muṭiyātu. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ peritum intu - airōppiya iṉattic cērntaviyē eṉṟu tirāviṭa moḻiyārāyccip pēraṟiñar ṭākṭar pōp karutukiṟār. “tamiḻk kic cuvaṭi”21 eṉṉum tam nūliṉ muṉṉuriyil avar piṉvarumāṟu eḻutiyuḷḷār: “teṉṉintiya moḻikaḷi evvaḷavukkevvaḷavu āḻntu paṭikkiṉṟōmō avvaḷavukkavvaḷavu avi vaṭamoḻit toṭarpuṭiyaṉaveṉavum, intu - airōppiya moḻiyiṉat tuṭaṉ mikavum neruṅkiya paṇṭit toṭarpu uṭiyaṉa veṉavum kāṇalām. āṉāl, avi veṟum pākataṅkaḷ eṉṟō, vaṭamoḻic citivukaḷ eṉṟō kūṟiviṭa muṭiyātu. avi intu - airōppiya moḻiyiṉattuḷ iṭam peṟa vēṇṭiyaṉavē eṉṟum, vaṭamoḻikku iṇiyāṉa vēṟoru tuṇimoḻiyiṉ citinta kūṟukaḷē avi eṉṟum, aviyum vaṭamoḻiyum orē paṇṭi mūla moḻiyiṉ kiḷikaḷ eṉṟum yām eppoḻutum karuti vantuḷōm. intu airōppiya moḻiyiṉattic cērnta kirēkkam, kātik22, persiyaṉ mutaliya moḻikaḷuṭaṉ avi neruṅkiya toṭarpuṭiyaṉa eṉpataṟku uṟatiyāṉa cāṉṟukaḷ pala uḷa; attakiya toṭarpu vaṭamoḻikku iruntatākac colvataṟ killi”. “malic coṟpoḻivu”23 eṉum viviliyap pakutiyit tirāviṭamoḻikaḷ nāṉkilum moḻipeyarttut tantuḷḷa tam nūliṉ muṉṉuriyil, pōp piṉvarumāṟu eḻutiyuḷḷār. “intat tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukkum keltik24 teyuttōṉiyam25 ākiya iru moḻikaḷukkum uḷḷa aṭippaṭiyāṉa āḻnta uṟavukaḷi moḻiyiyalār kūrntu nōkkutalvēṇṭum. iṭamum kālamum vāyppiṉ iv voppumikaḷi viritteḻutak kūṭum. in nūliṉ aṭutta patippilō vēṟu nūlilō itu paṟṟi mēl eḻutak karutiyuḷḷōm. muḻu oppumi akara varici oṉṟālaṉṟi vēṟetaṉālum im moḻikaḷiṉ iṉa uṟavi viḷakkutalaritu.” kaṭiciyākap pōp tamatu “tutamoḻi ilakkaṇac curukkat”26 tiṉ muṉṉuriyil piṉvarumāṟu kūṟukiṟār: “pēraṟiñar kālṭuveluṭaṉ yām peritum otta karuttuṭiyōm āyiṉum, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukkum, kelttiya moḻikaḷukkum iṭiyēyuḷḷa uṟavu iṉṉum naṉku ārāyak kiṭappatoṉṟeṉa eṇṇukiṉṟōm” eṉpatē. ittuṟi ārāycci mikavum iṉṟiyamiyātatē eṉpatil aiyamilli. pōp intu - airōppiya moḻiyuṭaṉē oṟṟumi, uṟavu, iṉa oṟṟumi eṉṟu kūṟuvaṉa vellām emakku uṭaṉpāṭē. in nūliṉ mutaṟpatippil avaṟṟi yāmē eṭuttukkāṭṭiyum uḷḷōm. āṉāl av eṭuttukkāṭṭukkaḷiliruntu pōp koṇṭa muṭipu em muṭīvāṉa muṭipuṭaṉ vēṟupaṭukiṟatu. ematu kōṭpāṭu cittiya, intu- airōppiya uṟavukaḷ iraṇṭaṟkum iṭan tarupaviyāka iruppiṉum, intu - airōppiya iṉattiviṭac cittiya iṉamē tirāviṭak kuḻuvōṭu neruṅkiya uyir niliyāṉa toṭarpu uṭiyatu eṉpatākum. kelttiya uṟavukaḷippaṟṟi eṭuttukkoṇṭāl, intu - airōppiya moḻikaḷ aṉittilum immoḻiyiṉamē potuppaṭac cittiya iṉattuṭaṉum, ciṟappākap piṉṉiyak kuḻuvuṭaṉum mika neruṅkiya toṭarpuṭiyaviyākat tōṉṟukiṟatu. kelttiyar airōppāvukku varumpōtu aṅkirunta muṉ kuṭikaḷ kelttik moḻiyiyum piṉṉiṣ moḻiyiyum pēci vantārkaḷō eṉpatu ārāyattakkatu. mēlum, kelttiya uṟavukaḷ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum uṟavukaḷ ciṟappākak kelttiya uṟavukaḷ tāṉā allatu uṇmiyil cittiya uṟavukaḷ tāṉā eṉpatum oppiṭṭu ārāyat takkatoṉṟām.
inta ārāycciyiṉ toṭakkattilēyē mēṟkuṟippiṭṭa tirāviṭa-intu=airōppiya oṟṟumikaḷ oppuk koḷḷap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ataṟkākak kuṟittuḷḷa eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷil cila oṟṟumikaḷ aṭippaṭiyāṉavi alla; ātalāl āḻntu nōkkum aḷavil maṟintuviṭukiṉṟaṉa. (tamiḻ oṉṟu allatu oṇṇu; ilattīṉam ūṉus; tamiḻ añcu; vaṭamoḻi pañca(ṉ); tamiḻ eṭṭu; vaṭamoḻi aṣṭa(ṉ) eṉpaṉa pōlpavi). ivaṟṟip puṟakkaṇittu viṭṭup pārttāluṅ kūṭat tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇa amitikaḷiṟ palavum, mutaṟ coṟkaḷiṟ palavum intu-airōppiya moḻikaḷuṭaṉ oppumiyuṭiyaviyāy irukkiṉṟaṉa. āyiṉum, uyir niliyāṉa amippu muḻuvatum aiyamaṟac cittiyac cārpuṭiya tātalāl cittiya iṉa uṟavē valiyuṟuttappaṭṭatu epirēya mutuc coṟkaḷiṟ pala vaṭamoḻi mutaṟcoṟkaḷuṭaṉ toṭarpuṭiyavi eṉṟu kāṭṭappaṭukiṉṟami karuti, epirēya moḻiyic cemittiya iṉattiṉiṉṟum pirittu intu - airōppiya moḻiyākak kaṇikkātatupōla, vaṭamoḻi, kirēkkam, kātik, kelttik, pārasīkam, mutaliya pala moḻikaḷuṭaṉ viyakkat takum oṟṟumikaḷ tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭiṉum tirāviṭa moḻikaḷic cittiya iṉattiliruntum pirikka vēṇṭiyatilli. “ciṉikō = āryaka” eṉṟa nūlil27 pēraṟiñar ṣlejel āriya cīṉa oppumikaḷic cuṭṭikkāṭṭi āriya cīṉa moḻikaḷ tammuḷ iṉa uṟavuṭiyaṉa eṉṟu viḷakka muyalkiṟār. avar kūṟiya uṟavu uṟutippaṭṭu viṭiṉuṅ kūṭac cīṉam āriyamoḻi eṉṟu āyviṭamāṭṭātu. iru moḻikaḷilum mikap paḻamiyāṉa cila mutaṟ coṟkaḷ uḷḷaṉa eṉṟutāṉ muṭivu ceyyappaṭum. mēlum cīṉamoḻi maṭṭumēyaṉṟi, vaṭamoḻiyum, epirēyamuṅkūṭa mutaṉmutalil ōraci moḻikaḷāy iruntaṉa eṉṟu ārāycciyāl teriyavarukiṉṟatu. perumpālum tirāviṭa mutaṟcoṟkaḷum aṅṅaṉam ōracic coṟkaḷē eṉpatu ivvārāycciyāl pulaṉākum. ivvakiyil pēraṟiñar pḷīk28 eṉpār āriya moḻikaḷ mikap paḻimiyāṉatoru kālattil, “tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ tākkutalkaḷukku uṭpaṭṭirukkavēṇṭum” eṉak koḷkiṟār. avar kūṟuvatāvatu:  “pāl vakuppuṭiya moḻikaḷuḷ ip potuppāl tiruntiya muṟiyil vaḻaṅkuvatu āṅkilam oṉṟilētāṉ. āṉāl paḻiya moḻikaḷuḷ āṅkilattiṟku oppat tiruttamāka itaṉit tirāviṭa moḻik kuḻuvē vaḻaṅki vantuḷḷatu. āṅkilamum tirāviṭa moḻikaḷum ip pāli vaḻaṅkum iṭaṅkaḷ, elli ākiyavaṟṟuḷ muṟṟilum ottirukkiṉṟaṉa. (āṇ, peṇ, potu eṉṟa) muppāl pākupāṭṭi uṭiya kuḻukkaḷ ivi iraṇṭumē eṉpati nōkka ivi uṟavaṟṟavi eṉṟu koḷḷutal iyal pākat tōṟṟavilli. pāl vakuppiyuṭiya āriya iṉattuṭaṉ tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ itukāṟum toṭarpupaṭuttappaṭa villiyāyiṉum, āriya moḻikaḷ uru aṭiyāmal vaḷarntu vanta kālattil avaṟṟil tirāviṭattākku ēṟpaṭṭataṉālēyē ivvoṟṟumiyum piṟa oṟṟumikaḷum ēṟpaṭṭirukkalām eṉṟu kūṟa iṭamuṇṭu” eṉpatē. ārāycci eṭṭumaḷavum pārttālum mikap paḻamiyāṉa kālattilkūṭat tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil ip potuppāl orumip (oṉṟaṉpāl) peyar iruntatākak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. āṉāl viṉiccollil pāl pākupāṭu ēṟpaṭṭatu piṟkālattilē eṉṟutāṉ eṇṇa vēṇṭum. intiyāvukkuḷ tirāviṭar varuṅkāl avarkaḷ moḻiyiṉ viṉiccollil vikutiyē iruntirukka iṭamilli; eṉavē, pāl pākupāṭum iruntirukka muṭiyātu. ulakil vēṟenta moḻikaḷiyumviṭat tirāviṭa moḻi viṉiccoṟkaḷilēyē pāl pākupāṭu niṟivuṭiyatāka irukkiṉṟatu eṉpatu piṉ teḷivuṟuttappaṭum. ittakiya pāl pākupāṭṭu vaḷarcci vaṭamoḻic cārpiṉāl uṇṭāṉa toṉṟākātu; ataṟku nērmāṟākavē uṇṭāyiruttal vēṇṭum.
tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil vaṭamoḻiyuṭaṉum intu - airōppiya moḻikaḷuṭaṉum toṭarpuṭiya cila vērc coṟkaḷum, col vaṭivaṅkaḷum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu potuppaṭa mēṟkūṟappaṭṭatu. tavaṟāṉa cila karuttu muṭipukaḷ ak kūṟṟiliruntu eḻakkūṭumātaliṉ, avaṟṟi vilakkutal iṉṟiyamiyātatākum. āriyarkaḷum tirāviṭarkaḷum pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ kalantu uṟintu vantamiyāl, vaṭamoḻi yiliruntu tiraviṭamoḻikaḷ pala  coṟkaḷik koṇṭu mēṟkūṟiya moḻiyoppumik karutti īṇṭu yām viḷakka muyalavilli. iru moḻikaḷiṉ aṭippaṭiyāṉa mūlac coṟkaḷilum, collamippilum kāṇappaṭum oṟṟumikaḷē yām eṭuttuk koṇṭaṉavākum. tammiyaṭuttu vaḻaṅkivanta vaḷamikka piṟamoḻikaḷiliruntu tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ pala coṟkaḷik kaṭaṉ vāṅkiyuḷḷaṉa eṉpatu oppukkoḷḷat takkatē; āṉāl atupōlavē vaṭamoḻiyum ciṟcila vakikaḷil tirāviṭa moḻic coṟkaḷik kaṭaṉ vāṅkip payaṉpaṭuttap piṉ vāṅkavilli eṉpatiyum oppuk koḷḷutal vēṇṭuma. potuvāka oru moḻiyiliruntu maṟṟoru moḻiyil ivvāṟu vantēṟiyuḷḷa coṟkaḷip pirittaṟital aritaṉṟu. eṭuttukkoṇṭa pala coṟkaḷum, collamippukaḷum vaṭamoḻiyōṭumaṭṭumaṉṟi intu-airōppiya moḻiyiṉattuṭaṉum oṟṟumiyuṭiyaṉavākak kāṇap peṟukiṉṟaṉa. iṉṉuṅ kūṟappōṉāl vaṭamoḻiyiṟ kāṇappeṟā oṟṟumikaḷ kirēkkattilum ilattīṉilum kāṇappeṟukiṉṟaṉa. vaṭamoḻiyil kāṇappeṟum oṟṟumi kaḷum ciṟcila iṭaṅkaḷil, tiruntiya amippuṭiya piṟkāla vaṭamoḻiyilaṉṟip (smskrutam) paṇṭiya tiruntāta vaṭamoḻiyilēyēyākum.
tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṟ kāṇappeṟum ittakiya koṭukkal vāṅkal cārpaṟṟa aṭippaṭiyāṉa oṟṟumikaḷiṟ cila kīḻē tarappaṭukiṉṟaṉa;
 (1) kirēkka moḻiyiṟpōl ṉakaram uṭampaṭu meyyākap payaṉpaṭutal.
(2) mūṉṟām iṭattilum (paṭarkkiyilum), viṉic collilum pāl pākupāṭu iruttal; ciṟappākap potuppāl (oṉṟaṉpāl) eṉa oṉṟiruttal.
(3) paṭarkki yorumi oṉṟaṉpāṟ peyariṉ vikuti `t’ āka iruttal.
(4) ilattīṉip pōṉṟu palaviṉpāl vikutī akaramā yiruttal.
(5) tolic cuṭṭu akaramākavum, aṇmiccuṭṭu ikaramākavum iruttal.
(6) pārasīka moḻi pōṉṟu iṟantakāla maṟivikkap perumpālum `t’ eṉṟa aṭiyi (iṭiniliyi)p payaṉpaṭuttal.
(7) pakutiyiṉ oru kūṟṟi iraṭṭittu iṟantakālam uṇartal.
(8) viṉippakutiyiṉ mutaluyir nīṇṭu toḻiṟ peyarātal.
mēṟkāṭṭiya cila pakutikaḷālēyē intu-airōppiya- tirāviṭat toṭarpu oṉṟu uḷḷatu eṉpatu pulappaṭum. aṉṟiyum, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukku arukiṟ paṉṉeṭunāḷāyp payiṉṟuvanta moḻi vaṭamoḻiyē yāyiṉum, tirāviṭa mutaṟ coṟkaḷiṟ pala vaṭamoḻi allatu kīḻa intu - airōppiyak kuḻuviviṭa mēla airōppiyak kuḻuviyē cārntaviyā yuḷḷaṉa eṉṟa kūṟṟiṉ uṇmiyum viḷaṅkum. eṉavē, tirāviṭa moḻiyic cittiya iṉac cārpuṭiyateṉa vakuttuk koḷvatē cariyāyiṉum, antac cittiya iṉattiluḷḷa maṟṟellā moḻikaḷiyum viṭa intu-airōppiya moḻi kaḷuṭaṉ mikap palavāṉa, paḻimiyāṉa ariya oppumikaḷi yuṭiya kuḻu tirāviṭak kuḻuvē eṉpatu teḷivākum. ik kuḻuvi āriya cittiya iṉaṅkaḷukku iṭippaṭṭa kuḻu eṉṟu collāmaṟ pōṉāluṅkūṭa, intu-airōppiya iṉattuṭaṉ uṟavuṭiya cittiya uṟuppu moḻi eṉṟēṉum kūṟat taṭiyilli. inta iraṇṭu iṉaṅkaḷiṉ mikap paḻimiyāṉa orumippāṭṭukkum ituvē cāṉṟākum. ik koḷki cariyāyiṉ, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiluḷḷa intu-airōppiya oṟṟumikaḷ nammi varalāṟṟuk kālaṅkaḷ aṉittiṟkum muṟpaṭṭa kālattiṟkuk koṇṭu cellukiṉṟaṉa; atumaṭṭumō? camayak katikaḷiṉ tōṟṟa kālattiṟkum muntiyum, intu-airōppiya iṉattār mēliiṉam, kīḻiiṉam eṉap pirivataṟkum muntiyum, intu - airōppiya iṉattāṉum, iṉṟu cittiyac cārpuṭiyavi eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum ellā moḻi kaḷukkum tāyakamāṉa mutaṟ cittiya moḻi yiṉattuṭaṉ oṉṟupaṭṭirunta kālattiṟku muntiyum koṇṭucellun takiyavā yirukkiṉṟaṉa.
tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil, ciṟappākat tamiḻil, orucila cemittiya oṟṟumikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟami viyakkattakkatē. avaṟṟuḷḷum cila oṟṟumikaḷ ivviraṇṭil maṭṭumaṉṟi, epirēyattilum, intu-airōppiya moḻikaḷilum irukkiṉṟaṉa. ittakiya iṭaṅkaḷil epirēya uruvē intu-airōppiya uruviṉum tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukku aṇmiyatā yirukkiṉṟatu. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka, tamiḻil avā (peyarccol); āval (toḻiṟ peyar); ivaṟṟi ilattīṉam ave-ō (viṉippakuti), vaṭamoḻi av mutaliyavi aṇṭiyē irukkiṉṟaṉa. āyiṉum, epirēyam avvāh (peyarccol), āvah (viṉi) eṉṟa iraṇṭum mikavum aṇmiyuṭiyavā yirukkiṉṟami kāṇka! iṅṅaṉam tirāviṭam uḷḷaṭaṅkiya ellā iṉaṅkaḷukkum potuvāṉa oṟṟumikaḷ orupuṟam irukka, tirāviṭam, epirēyam ivaṟṟiṟkē ciṟappāy, piṟa moḻikaḷ evaṟṟiṟkum uriyavallāta oppumip paṇpukaḷum cila irukkiṉṟaṉa.
mēṟ kuṟippiṭṭa tamiḻiluḷḷa cemittiya oṟṟumikaḷ intu - airōppiya oṟṟumikaḷip pōlac ciṟantaviyumalla, palavu malla; āṉāl, im mūṉṟiṉaṅkaḷiluṅ kāṇappeṟum oṟṟumikaḷuṭaṉum, epirēyattuṭaṉ mikavum neruṅkiyavāyk kāṇappeṟum paṇpukaḷuṭaṉum cērntu, avi tirāviṭa moḻiyiṉ paṇṭit toṉmiyip paṟṟi ārāyum aṟiñarukku mikavum payaṉ tarat takkavi. ittakiya oppumi kaḷi iṅkē kuṟippatu tirāviṭattiṟkum epirēyattiṟkum ivaṟṟiṟkum toṭarpu uṇṭeṉṟu kāṭṭavaṉṟu; “pala maram kaṇṭa taccaṉ oru maramum veṭṭamāṭṭāṉ” eṉṟa uṇmiyik karuttiṟ koṇṭu ittuṟikaḷil ellām ārāycciyic celuttāviṭiṉum, piṟaratu ārāyccikkāka ittakiya muṭivu paṭuttappaṭāta citaṟiya oppumikaḷi appaṭiyē eṭuttuk kāṭṭavēyām. āṉāl, intu- airōppiya tirāviṭa oppumikaḷ moḻiyiyaliṉ uyirniliyi oṭṭiyaviyātaliṉ avaṟṟip paḻimiyāṉa kūṭṭuṟaviṉāl peṟappaṭṭavi eṉṟu kūṟiviṭa muṭiyātu. cemittiya oṟṟumikaḷō iṅṅaṉamaṉṟi iṉa oṟṟumiyi iṉṟiyamiyātaviyāka vēṇṭakkūṭiyavi yalla; taṟceyalāy ēṟpaṭṭavi eṉṟō, allatu ētēṉum orukālattil paṇṭit tirāviṭarkaḷ cemittiya iṉattāruṭaṉ aṭuttu vāḻntamiyāl ēṟpaṭṭavi (itu eḷitil iruntirukkak kūṭiyatē) eṉṟō kūṟiviṭalām.
iṉi, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ iṭappeyarkaḷ teṟku, mēṟku āstirēliyāviluḷḷa paḻañkuṭikaḷiṉ moḻiyiṟ kāṇappeṟum iṭappeyarkaḷi viyakkattakka muṟiyil ottirukkiṉṟaṉa. iv voppumikkuk kāraṇaṅkaḷ pala kūṟalāmāyiṉum, oppumi irukkiṉṟa teṉpati maṭṭilum maṟuttaṟkilli. āṉāl, āstirēliyat taṉmi iṭappeyar, tirāviṭamoḻit taṉmi iṭappeyariviṭa, tipēttiya moḻit taṉmip peyari mikutiyum ottuḷḷatu eṉpatu itukāṟuṅ kavaṉikkappaṭavilli. kīḻvarum oppumip paṭṭiyi nōkkuka!
	tirāviṭam 	āsti	tipēttiyam	cīṉam			rēliyam
taṉmi	nāṉ,yāṉ	ṅa,ṅii,	ṅa,ṅe,ṅet,	ṅo
orumi	nā,eṉ	ṅatsa,ṅaṉya	
itil āstirēliyat taṉmip peyariṉ pakuti tipēttiya moḻiyiyum intu-cīṉa iṉattiyum peritum ottiruppiṉum, paṉmiyākum poḻuta atu tirāviṭa moḻikaḷi, atilum ciṟappākat teluṅku moḻiyi, ottirukkiṟatu. teluṅkil paṉmi lu eṉṟa vikuti cērppatāl ēṟpaṭukiṟatu. atēpōṉṟu āstirēliya moḻikaḷilum lu, li, ṭalu, ṭli ivaṟṟic cērppatāl paṉmi ēṟpaṭukiṟatu. intiyāviṉ vaṭakiḻakkiluḷḷa moḻikaḷum ivvakiyil teluṅki ottuḷḷaṉa. eṭuttuk kāṭṭāka, timal29 nā (= nī) eṉpataṉ paṉmi niyel (=nīr, nīṅkaḷ) eṉpatām. taṉmiyilum āstirēliya moḻiyiṉ irumip peyarkaḷ, paṉmip peyarkaḷ ṅalu, ṅaṭlu, ṅaṭli, ṅalata eṉpaṉa pōṉṟavi . itaṉuṭaṉ teluṅkup peyarkaḷ paṉmiyākum muṟiyik kūrntu nōkkuka. (e-ṭu) : vā(ṇ)ṭu (=avaṉ), vā(ṇ)ṭlu (=avarkaḷ); tamiḻiluṅkūṭa `nāṉ’ eṉpataṉ paṉmiyāṉa `nāṅkaḷ’ itaṉi mikavum aṭuttiruppatu kāṇka!
āstirēliya muṉṉili iṭappeyarkaḷum, tirāviṭa muṉṉili iṭappeyarkaḷum taṉmi, paṉmi iraṇṭilum teḷivāka ottirukkiṉṟaṉa. atilum vikutiyuṭaṉmaṭṭumaṉṟip pakutiyilum muḻu oṟṟumi iruppatuṅ kāṇka! tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil potuppaṭiyāṉa (muṉṉilip) peyarkaḷiṉ orumi nīṉ eṉpatum, paṉmi nīm eṉpatum ām. ivaṟṟuḷ nī eṉpatē pakutip poruḷ taruvatu. i/tu īreṇkaḷilum oṉṟākavē irukkiṟatu. orumiyil orumi vikutiyāka ṉakaramum (ṉ), paṉmiyil paṉmi vikutiyāka makaramum (m) varukiṉṟaṉa. cila iṭaṅkaḷil paṉmi vikutiyākiya marakattiṟku māṟākap paṭarkkip paṉmiyiṉ vikutiyākiya rakaram vantu nīr (teluṅku mīru; atāvatu mīr+u) ātalum uṇṭu. āṉāl, putitāka vanta inta rakara vikuti eḻuvāyil, atilum taṉiyāṉacollil maṭṭumē varukiṟatu. piṟa vikutikaḷ urupukaḷ cērnta pakupataṅkaḷuḷ varumpoḻutu makara vikutiyē varutal kāṇka! (e-ṭu): nāṅkaḷ = nām +kaḷ; nīṅkaḷ = nīm+kaḷ; uṅkaḷ ummuṭiya mutalāyiṉa. orumiyil muṉṉilip peyariluḷḷa uyiroli ikaramēyāyiṉum, paṉmiyil ikarattiṉiṭamāka ukaramum varukiṟatu. (e-ṭu): pirakuvi; num = nīṅkaḷ; centamiḻ: num (uṅkaḷuṭiya). ik kālak kaṉṉaṭam in “nīm” eṉpataṉ makaratti vakaramāka melivittu, nīu30 allatu nīvu31 ākkukiṉṟatu kāṇka! immāṟṟaṅkaḷ ovvoṉṟilum ivvāṟu āstirēliya moḻiyiyalpu tirāviṭa moḻi yiyalpi muṟṟilum ottiruppatu viḷakka muṭiyātatoru putumiyēyām.
	tirāviṭam 	āstirēliyam
muṉṉili		niṉṉa,ṅiṉṉe,
orumi	nīṉ, niṉ	ṅintoa, niṅte.
muṉṉilip	nīm,nim	nimetū, nura,	paṉmi	nīr, num	niva, ṅurle.			nīvu
kīḻvarum oppumippaṭṭiyil irumi, paṉmi ākiya īreṇkaḷum (iraṇṭiṉ poruḷum tirāviṭamoḻikaḷil oṉṟēyātalāl) paṉmi eṉṟē kuṟikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
tamiḻ taṉmi orumi iraṇṭām vēṟṟumi urupu `eṉṉi’ eṉpatiyum āstirēliya iraṇṭām vēṟṟumi uru `eṉmo’ eṉpatiyum oppunōkkuka!
āstirēliya moḻikaḷiṉ moḻiyamippu cittiya iṉattuṭaṉ perumpālum oṟṟumiyuṭiyatākavē kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.muṉṉaṭikaḷiṉ iṭamākap piṉṉaṭikaḷi vaḻaṅkutal; taṉmip paṉmiyil oṉṟu muṉṉili nīṅkalākavum oṉṟu ataṉi uḷappaṭuttiyum iraṇṭu vaṭivaṅkaḷ uṭimi; taṉviṉi, piṟaviṉi mutaliyavaṟṟi vikuti cērppatālēyē terivittal; coṟkaḷiṉ aṭukkiyal amippu, vākkiyat tiluḷḷa coṟṟoṭarpu muṟi ākiya ivi yaṉittilum ivi tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiyum, turukkiyam maṅkōliyam pōṉṟa piṟa cittiya moḻikaḷiyum ottē irukkiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl, oṉṟiraṇṭu iṭaṅkaḷ nīṅkalāka, `pāliṉēsiya’32 moḻikaḷuṭaṉ ivi vēṟupaṭukiṉṟaṉa. āstirēliya moḻikaḷil tiraṭṭappaṭṭa coṟṟokutikaḷil ivvoṟṟumikaḷi uṟutippaṭuttum vēṟu vivaraṅkaḷ illiyāyiṉum, ivvoṟṟumikaḷ taṟceyalāṉavi yeṉṟu viṭattakkaviyalla.
naṭu āppirikkāvil pōrṉu33 nāṭṭil pēcappaṭum kaṉūri34 allatu pōrṉu eṉṉum moḻikkum tirāviṭa moḻikkum toṭarpiruppatu mēṟkūṟiya oppumiyiṉum putumiyāṉatēyām. iv voppumikaḷ potuppaṭi yāṉavi eṉpatu uṇmiyē. aviyāvaṉa: kaṉūriyum “aṭukkiyal” amip puṭiyatu uṟavu aṭikaḷi muṉṉaṭiyākkāmal piṉṉaṭi yākkukiṟatu; aiyam, viṉā, aḻuttam mutaliyavaṟṟik kuṟikka (tirāviṭamoḻi kaḷukkē ciṟappāṉa muṟiyil) aṭikaḷip peyar viṉikaḷuṭaṉ cērkkiṟatu; viṉikku etirmaṟi uruvum uṇṭu haṅkēriya moḻiyippōṉṟu taṉviṉi35 piṟaviṉi36 uṇṭu. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷuṭaṉ mika neruṅkiya oṟṟumi iruppatik kuṟippatu muṉṉili orumiyākiya `ni’ eṉpatākum. ituvum tirāviṭam pirākuvi, cīṉam, pehistaṉ aṭṭavaṇi moḻi, āstirēliyam mutaliya moḻikaḷ aṉittiṟkum potuvāṉateṉpatu mēlē kāṭṭappaṭṭatu. kaṉūri moḻi mēla āppirikkāviṉ moḻikaḷiliruntu mikutiyum vēṟṟumip paṭuvatāl atu taṉippaṭṭa ārāyccikkuriyatākum.37
